## riftworld_leader_description

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ariftworld_leader_description&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!被从星界裂隙的另一头带回来到我们的母行星附近，它也是平行时空里我们远古母星中的一员。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:6`

### English

Brought back from beyond the Astral Rift near our home planet, §Y[Root.GetName]§! is a member of our ancestral homeworld in a parallel dimension.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:8`

## spirit_of_ancestors_modifier_desc

## astral_rift.1.desc.common.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1.desc.common.1&lang=en)

### 中文

向[explorer.capital_scope.GetName]附近的裂隙中的航行将我们引向了一颗残破行星的轨道上。\n\n一进入大气层，布满燃烧的弹坑的景色映入眼帘，半截埋入黄沙的战舰四散其间。这颗星球就是一片战场。\n\n截获的无线电信号疯狂而令人不安。难以置信的是，

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:12`

### English

Our voyage into the rift near [explorer.capital_scope.GetName] has delivered us into the orbit of a planet in ruins.\n\nUpon entering the atmosphere, we discovered a landscape pock-marked with burning craters. Here and there, warships lay half buried in the sands. This world is a battlefield.\n\nIntercepted radio transmissions are frenzied and unnerving. Incredibly,

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:16`

## astral_rift.1.desc.no_arcsite

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1.desc.no_arcsite&lang=en)

### 中文

当地居民似乎使用我们语言的一种远古版本进行交流。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:13`

### English

the inhabitants appear to be communicating in an archaic version of our own language.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:17`

## astral_rift.1.desc.with_arcsite

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1.desc.with_arcsite&lang=en)

### 中文

当地居民的语言与我们从[explorer.capital_scope.GetName]地下的远古球体中所回收的文档若合符节。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:14`

### English

the language of the inhabitants matched precisely with the documents we recovered from the ancient sphere beneath [explorer.capital_scope.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:18`

## astral_rift.1.desc.common.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.1.desc.common.2&lang=en)

### 中文

粗略的翻译表明这些人在一场旷日持久的冲突中失败了。他们的敌人，文中仅简单地称之为“侵略者”，则正在准备下一场进攻。鉴于这颗行星的破坏度，这应该也是最后一场了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:15`

### English

Rough translations indicate these people are on the losing side of an enduring conflict. Their foes, referred to simply as "The Invaders", are preparing another wave of attack. Given the devastation on the planet, it may well be the last.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:19`

## astral_rift.3.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.3.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

在这颗行星的地表有数个大型人口中心。从他们的通讯内容中我们辨别出了两个截然不同的意识形态派系。\n\n§Y[GetStarlightVanguardName]§!是坚定的军国主义者，准备以万众之志抵御即将到来的战争。\n\n§Y黑幕派§!则是一种教权秩序，他们在迫在眉睫的威胁面前允诺灵魂的安宁。\n\n现在，我们很确定如此相似的语言绝非巧合。这就是我们活生生的祖先，而无论哪个派系现在都岌岌可危。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:20`

### English

There are several large population centers on the surface of the planet. Based on their transmissions, we have identified two distinct ideological factions.\n\nThe §Y[GetStarlightVanguardName]§! are grim militarists, preparing for the coming conflict with unified determination.\n\nThe §YBlack Curtain§! are a priestly order, promising spiritual sanctuary from the looming threat.\n\nAt this point, we are certain that the similarities in our languages cannot be mere coincidence. These are our living ancestors, and both factions are in danger.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:25`

## astral_rift.3.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.3.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.3.desc.default$\n\n这些人与我们书同文，面同相，却具有自主意识。这怎么可能呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:21`

### English

$astral_rift.3.desc.default$\n\nThese people use our language. Their physiognomy is nearly identical to our own. Yet they possess individual minds. How can that be?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:26`

## astral_rift.3.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.3.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一些自主单元！他们与网络意识大相径庭，每个个体都有独立的运算和决策能力。在这颗行星的表面有许多大型的人口中心。基于他们的通讯内容，我们甚至辨识出了两个截然不同的意识形态阵营。\n\n§Y[GetStarlightVanguardName]§!是坚定的军国主义者，准备以万众之志抵御即将到来的战争。\n\n§Y黑幕派§!则是一种教权秩序，他们在迫在眉睫的威胁面前允诺灵魂的安宁。\n\n我们的祖先现在岌岌可危了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:22`

### English

These are individual units! Far from a networked consciousness, each one has its own processing power and decision making capabilities. There are several large population centers on the surface of the planet. Based on their transmissions, we have identified two distinct ideological factions.\n\nThe §Y[GetStarlightVanguardName]§! are grim militarists, preparing for the coming conflict with unified determination.\n\nThe §YBlack Curtain§! are a priestly order, promising spiritual sanctuary from the looming threat.\n\nOur predecessors are in danger of being wiped out.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:27`

## astral_rift.5.common_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.5.common_1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的语言、面相及§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!的存在让他们很快确信了我们的承袭关系。他们在无比急切沟通中教会了我们许多历史知识。\n\n很久以前，我们的物种面临着一种异次元威胁。这些侵略者一来便展开了血腥的大清洗。我们的人民起先坚定反抗，但防线在日复一日的侵蚀中逐渐瓦解。\n\n在被击败的前夕，一道星界裂隙出现了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:29`

### English

Our language, appearance, and the presence of a §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! quickly convinced them of our common heritage. In their desperation to communicate, they revealed much about our history.\n\nLong ago, our species was confronted by an extradimensional threat. The invaders arrived to wage a brutal extermination campaign. At first our people stood firm, but over time their defenses crumbled.\n\nOn the eve of defeat, an Astral Rift appeared.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:35`

## astral_rift.5.militarist_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.5.militarist_2&lang=en)

### 中文

为了尝试从裂隙中逃生，为整个文明留下星星之火，“救生艇”行动开始了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:30`

### English

Operation "Life Boat" was launched in a bid to escape through the rift and preserve what remained of their civilization.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:36`

## astral_rift.5.spiritualist_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.5.spiritualist_2&lang=en)

“奇迹之门”被视为逃生并保留他们生活方式的最后机会。

### English

## astral_rift.5.no_arcsite

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:32`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:38`

## astral_rift.5.arcsite

$astral_rift.5.no_arcsite$这也就是在[explorer.capital_scope.GetName]的远古深坑中埋藏的那台。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:33`

### English

$astral_rift.5.no_arcsite$ This was the very object we found buried deep inside the ancient crater in [explorer.capital_scope.GetName].

## astral_rift.5.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.5.default&lang=en)

### 中文

一群精挑细选的“难民”开始为这项任务做准备。他们就是我们的祖先。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:34`

### English

A select group of refugees was prepped for the mission. These were our ancestors.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:40`

## astral_rift.5.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.5.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

为了在这趟旅程中幸存下来，这群适任者被进行了深度的改造。我们的祖先相信只有统一的思想才有希望能安稳地穿过裂隙。这也就是我们意识的真正起源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:35`

### English

In order to withstand the journey, a select group of compatible individuals were radically transformed. Our ancestors believed that only a unified mind could hope to pass through the rift unscathed. This was the very beginning of our consciousness.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:41`

## astral_rift.5.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.5.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

为了在这段旅途中幸存下来，我们的先祖将他们的意识链接到一起并摒弃了情绪。我们的意识便从这种清晰的视野和团结的目标之中诞生来下来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:36`

### English

In order to survive the journey, our progenitors linked their minds and purged their emotions. With a clear vision and a unified purpose our consciousness was formed.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:42`

## astral_rift.5.common_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.5.common_2&lang=en)

### 中文

在裂隙的这头，球体在短短的15年后便发射升空。这里的许多人们谈起那天仿佛还历历在目，而对于我们却已经渡过了数个纪元。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:37`

### English

On this side of the rift, the Sphere was launched less than fifty years ago. There are some among the people here who still remember that day. For us, eons have passed.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:43`

## astral_rift.10.militarist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.10.militarist&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与“§Y[GetStarlightVanguardName]§!”召开了会议，他们平铺直述地表明了对抗侵略的局势之严峻。异次元体舰队战在数个星系之外就被探测到了，目前已经近在咫尺。我们在这么一个节骨眼上出现，他们相信这绝非偶然。我们的出现就是命运的造化。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:53`

### English

We held council with the "§Y[GetStarlightVanguardName]§!". They have made plain how grim things stand in their war against the Invaders. Extradimensional fleets have been detected several systems away and are already closing in. We have arrived at a critical moment. To the Vanguard this is no coincidence. Our presence is an act of fate.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:61`

## astral_rift.10.spiritualist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.10.spiritualist&lang=en)

### 中文

“§Y[GetStarlightVanguardName]§!”同意与我们召开会议。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:54`

### English

The "§Y[GetStarlightVanguardName]§!" has agreed to hold council with us.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:62`

## astral_rift.10.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.10.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

“你们的到来让我们的人民为之一振。你们的雪中送炭，让我们终于有希望彻底结束这场战争。”他们的剩余舰队早已破败不堪，再也承受不起一丁点损失。\n\n我们可以在裂隙能保持稳定的限度内，通过锚缆传输不多的补给物资。供给一批能解燃眉之急的合金能解决舰队的维修和升级问题。他们建造的舰队更加先进，我们也可以在这个过程中学到相关的设计。\n\n因为时间有限，更明智的选择则是将我们对星界丝缕的深刻理解用于增强他们舰队的护盾。虽然这只是一种实验性的举措，但在这里取得的突破也可以使我们自己的舰队受益匪浅。据我们所知，这些入侵者偏好能量武器，所以这里的任何改进都会大有帮助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:55`

### English

"Your arrival has inspired our people. Give us the critical aid we need, and we yet have a chance to end this war once and for all." Their remaining fleets are in poor condition and cannot withstand another loss.\n\nWe should be able to send a limited amount of supplies through the anchor cable before the rift becomes too unstable. A shipment of direly needed alloys will help with fleet repairs and enhancements. Given the advanced construction of their ships, we may learn something of their designs along the way.\n\nA wiser use of our time may be to apply our more advanced knowledge of astral thread into enhancing their ship's shields. While experimental in nature, we may make some breakthroughs here that could benefit our own fleets. It is our understanding that the invaders favor energy weapons, so any improvements here would go a long way.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:63`

## astral_rift.15.spiritualist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.15.spiritualist&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与“§Y黑幕派§!”举行了一场特别的会谈。他们让我们对侵略者的可怕生性印象深刻。当探测到异次元体舰队的时候他们已经在来的路上了。对黑幕派，我们在这个节骨眼上的到来正是天意。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:64`

### English

We held a special council with the "§YBlack Curtain§!". They have impressed upon us the horrifying nature of the Invaders. Extradimensional fleets have been detected and are already on their way. To the Curtain, our arrival at this critical moment is an act of divine providence.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:73`

## astral_rift.15.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.15.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

“你们的到来宛如奇迹。我们曾已经对这场无望的战争认命。我们的人民正在忍饥挨饿，苦难深重。门后之子们……请求你们在我们的最后时光给予我们恩慈。”\n\n困境显而易见，而未来却一片迷茫。我们所能做的则是喂饱他们的人民：我们可以通过锚缆传输一些有限的补给，而我们先进的农业科技绝对能派上大用场。\n\n甚至还有机会能用§H星界丝缕§!注入他们的飞船后，安全地接走一些他们的人。成功的概率不大，但只要成功至少能提振他们的精神。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:65`

### English

"Your arrival is nothing less than a miracle. Long ago we accepted that this war as hopeless. Our people are starving, and our suffering is great. Children from beyond the Door... Lend us your charity in our last moments."\n\nTheir plight is obvious, even if their fate is not. Feeding the people is within our means: we should be able to send limited supplies through the anchor cable, and our advanced agricultural techniques will surely prove useful.\n\nThere is even a chance that we could shuttle some of their people to safety through the rift by infusing §HAstral Threads§! into the fuel of their spacecraft. It is a small chance, but one that may at the very least raise their spirits.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:74`

## astral_rift.15.desc.lithoid

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.15.desc.lithoid&lang=en)

### 中文

“你们的到来宛如奇迹。我们曾已经对这场无望的战争认命。我们的人民正在忍饥挨饿，苦难深重。门后之子们……请求你们在我们的最后时光给予我们恩慈。”\n\n困境显而易见，而未来却一片迷茫。我们所能做的则是喂饱他们的人民：我们可以通过锚缆传输一些有限的补给，而我们先进的矿业科技绝对能派上大用场。\n\n甚至还有机会能用§H星界丝缕§!注入他们的飞船后，安全地接走一些他们的人。成功的概率不大，但只要成功至少能提振他们的精神。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:66`

### English

"Your arrival is nothing less than a miracle. Long ago we accepted that this war as hopeless. Our people are starving, and our suffering is great. Children from beyond the Door... Lend us your charity in our last moments."\n\nTheir plight is obvious, even if their fate is not. Feeding the people is within our means: we should be able to send limited supplies through the anchor cable, and our advanced mineral extraction will surely prove useful.\n\nThere is even a chance that we could shuttle some of their people to safety through the rift by infusing §HAstral Threads§! into the fuel of their spacecraft. It is a small chance, but one that may at the very least raise their spirits.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:75`

## astral_rift.15.desc.synthetic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.15.desc.synthetic&lang=en)

### 中文

“你们的到来宛如奇迹。我们曾已经对这场无望的战争认命。我们的人民已经耗尽了能量，苦难深重。门后之子们……请求你们在我们的最后时光给予我们恩慈。”\n\n困境显而易见，而未来却一片迷茫。我们所能做的则是用能量喂饱他们的人民：我们可以通过锚缆传输一些有限的补给，而我们先进的能源科技绝对能派上大用场。\n\n甚至还有机会能用§H星界丝缕§!注入他们的飞船后，安全地接走一些他们的人。成功的概率不大，但只要成功至少能提振他们的精神。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:67`

### English

"Your arrival is nothing less than a miracle. Long ago we accepted that this war as hopeless. Our people are running out of power, and our suffering is great. Children from beyond the Door... Lend us your charity in our last moments."\n\nTheir plight is obvious, even if their fate is not. Powering the people is within our means: we should be able to send limited supplies through the anchor cable, and our advanced power generators will surely prove useful.\n\nThere is even a chance that we could shuttle some of their people to safety through the rift by infusing §HAstral Threads§! into the fuel of their spacecraft. It is a small chance, but one that may at the very least raise their spirits.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:76`

## astral_rift.15.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.15.c&lang=en)

### 中文

这些努力是徒劳的。我们去问问那些军事主义者吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:78`

### English

These efforts are hopeless. Let us visit the militants.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:87`

## astral_rift.15.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.15.d&lang=en)

### 中文

这是在以卵击石。我们还是回到自己的新世界吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:79`

### English

This is a lost cause. Let us return to the new world.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:88`

## astral_rift.16.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.16.name&lang=en)

### 中文

抛弃先祖

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:80`

### English

Abandoning Our Ancestors

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:90`

## astral_rift.16.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.16.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

我们最终遗憾地确定，先祖的境遇已经是无可救药了。\n\n我们对此做什么都于事无补。他们将我们通过裂隙送到这个次元的决定是正确的。\n\n我们会继承他们的意志好好地活下去，成为一个足以让他们感到骄傲的文明。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:81`

### English

We have decided that the plight of our ancestors is, unfortunately, a lost cause.\n\nThere is little we can do to help them. They made the right decision in sending a portion of themselves through the rift and into our dimension.\n\nWe will do our best to continue their legacy and become a civilization they would be proud of.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:91`

## astral_rift.16.desc.homicidal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.16.desc.homicidal&lang=en)

### 中文

我们判定，先祖不值得我们的拯救。\n\n他们将我们这些最强的后代通过裂隙送到这个次元是一个正确的决定。我们会替他们好好活下去。\n\n他们现在需要独自面对最终命运了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:82`

### English

We have judged our ancestors as unworthy of our support.\n\nThey made the right decision in sending their strongest through the rift and into our dimension. We will live on in their stead.\n\nTheir fate is now their own.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:92`

## astral_rift.20.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.20.name&lang=en)

### 中文

星球议会

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:83`

### English

World Council

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:94`

## astral_rift.20.common_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.20.common_1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们祖先的母星将面临的入侵迫在眉睫。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:84`

### English

The invasion on our ancestor's home planet is imminent.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:95`

## astral_rift.20.militarist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.20.militarist&lang=en)

### 中文

在与“§Y[GetStarlightVanguardName]§!”的会谈之后，我们受邀出席星球议会召开的一场紧急会议。他们强调了我们目前为止所给予的帮助，对我们的慷慨大加称赞。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:85`

### English

Following our meeting with the "§Y[GetStarlightVanguardName]§!", we have been invited to a world council for an emergency meeting. They took special note of the aid we have given so far, and praised us for our generosity.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:96`

## astral_rift.20.spiritualist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.20.spiritualist&lang=en)

### 中文

在与“§Y黑幕派§!”的会谈之后，我们受邀出席星球议会召开的一场紧急会议。他们强调了我们目前为止所给予的帮助，对我们的慷慨大加称赞。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:86`

### English

Following our meeting with the "§YBlack Curtain§!", we were invited to a world council for an emergency meeting. They remain grateful for the aid we have given so far, praising us for our generosity.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:97`

## astral_rift.20.common_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.20.common_2&lang=en)

### 中文

在被视为最终之战到来之前我们已经没有多少行动的时间了。\n\n能源库存已经岌岌可危。捐献一些能量币将会促进紧急支援舰船的生产，把战争推向有利的一面。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:87`

### English

There is little time for action before what many are viewing as the final conflict.\n\nStockpiles of energy are dangerously low. Contributing a donation would boost the production of emergency support ships and push the battle in their favor.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:98`

## astral_rift.20.poor

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.20.poor&lang=en)

### 中文

他们的许多科学家还向我们申请用于进一步研究增强他们舰队护盾的星界丝缕的样品。因为这些入侵者偏好能量武器，好处是显而易见的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:88`

### English

Many of their scientists are also requesting samples of astral thread we have collected for further experimentation in enhancing the shielding of their fleets. Since these invaders tend to favor energy weapons, the benefits here could potentially be significant.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:99`

## astral_rift.20.rich

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.20.rich&lang=en)

### 中文

他们的许多科学家还向我们申请更多用于进一步研究增强他们舰队护盾的星界丝缕的样品。因为这些入侵者偏好能量武器，好处是显而易见的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:89`

### English

Many of their scientists are also requesting more samples of astral thread we have collected for further experimentation in enhancing the shielding of their fleets. Since these invaders tend to favor energy weapons, the benefits here could potentially be significant.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:100`

## astral_rift.20.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.20.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

看着我们祖先在谈判桌上的表现——那种个人观点的混乱表达——简直是一种折磨。幸亏这种低效的东西在长久之前就已经被从我们身上编辑掉了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:90`

### English

Watching our ancestors engage in debate - the chaotic expression of individual opinions - is unsettling. We must be thankful that such inefficiency was engineered out of us long ago.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:101`

## astral_rift.20.b

### 中文

## astral_rift.25.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.25.name&lang=en)

### 中文

欢庆

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:102`

### English

Celebration

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:114`

## astral_rift.25.common_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.25.common_1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的先祖舰队成功地击退了入侵者。全球各地爆发起了一阵有一阵的欢庆热潮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:103`

### English

Our ancestral fleets have successfully repelled the Invaders. Wild celebrations have erupted all across the planet.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:115`

## astral_rift.25.thread

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.25.thread&lang=en)

### 中文

我们为战斗提供的星界丝缕对舰队的燃料供给和亚光速性能的增益卓有成效。这些实验性结果也可以应用到我们自己的舰队上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:104`

### English

The astral thread we provided for the battle proved to be a useful supplement to the fleet's fuel supplies and boosted sub-light performance. This experimental application can be used for our own armada.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:116`

## astral_rift.25.energy

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.25.energy&lang=en)

### 中文

我们供给的能量对填补后勤系统漏洞的紧急支援舰队的建造至关重要。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:105`

### English

The energy we provided was key in producing emergency support fleets that were used to fill critical holes in their logistics chains.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:117`

## astral_rift.25.common_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.25.common_2&lang=en)

### 中文

星球议会以我们命名了一个全球节日。未来的子子孙孙都将在这一天庆祝[explorer.GetName]日。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:106`

### English

The world council is declaring a planetary holiday in our name. [explorer.GetName] Day will be celebrated for generations to come.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:118`

## astral_rift.25.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.25.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

这里的许多人都羡慕于我们紧密一致的意识。有些人甚至建议将他们自己的思维按照我们成功的范例进行编辑。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:107`

### English

Many here look upon our bonded consciousness with envy. Some even suggest they begin to engineer their own minds to follow our worthy example.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:119`

## astral_rift.25.common_3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.25.common_3&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以从这个社会中遴选一位杰出人士作为大使和历史学家带走。其余的祖先则会继续在这里重建家园。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:108`

### English

We may choose one prominent figure from this society to bring back with us as an ambassador and historian. The rest of our ancestors shall remain and rebuild.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:120`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.30.name&lang=en)

### 中文

荒废度

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:121`

### English

Devastation

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:134`

## astral_rift.30.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.30.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

一位杰出贵族私下联系我们，希望以一大笔能量币报酬换取我们保护[riftworld_noble_leader.GetHisHer]的人身安全。\n\n该帝国内容最全面图书馆的管理员恳求我们取回他们大量的书籍和数据磁盘来保存。\n\n我们必须做出取舍，因为入侵者可不会等我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:122`

### English

One prominent noble has asked us privately to ensure [riftworld_noble_leader.GetHisHer] survival and offers a large amount of energy credits in return.\n\nCaretakers of the empire's most comprehensive library of knowledge plead for us to instead take back their vast collection of books and data disks for preservation.\n\nWe must make a choice, for the invaders come to this place in due time.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:135`

## astral_rift.30.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.30.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

惨败在所难免。\n\n我们先祖的舰队面对入侵者毫无胜算。入侵者武器骇人且毫无慈悲。现在先祖们已经几乎没有立锥之地了，人们惊慌失措地恳求我们把他们从裂隙中带走逃生。\n\n$astral_rift.30.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:123`

### English

The carnage was absolute.\n\nOur ancestral fleets stood no chance against the invaders. Their weapons are terrible, and their mercy is none. Few bastions of safety now remain. The people are panicking and begging to flee back with us back through the rift.\n\n$astral_rift.30.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:136`

## astral_rift.30.desc.homicidal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.30.desc.homicidal&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的先祖被入侵者们轻易地击败了，很快将面临种族灭绝。他们已经坦然接受了失败，等待着终结的降临。\n\n$astral_rift.30.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:124`

### English

Our ancestors were easily bested by the invaders and will soon succumb to extermination. They have accepted the shame of defeat and now await the end.\n\n$astral_rift.30.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:137`

## astral_rift.30.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.30.a&lang=en)

### 中文

尽我们所能带走一些。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:125`

### English

Take as many back as we can.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:138`

## astral_rift.30.a.trigger_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.30.a.trigger_tt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可没有兴趣保留我们先祖中的最弱者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:126`

### English

We hold no interest in preserving the weakest of our ancestors.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:139`

### English

## astral_rift.40.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.40.name&lang=en)

### 中文

先祖之魂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:131`

### English

Spirit of our Ancestors

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:145`

## astral_rift.40.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.40.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

先祖的发现在我们的社会中掀起了轩然大波。\n\n我们是一个远古文明的后裔，我们拾取了他们宝贵知识和技术专长的牙慧。这趟裂隙之旅具有里程碑式的意义，将会被长久铭记。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:132`

### English

The discovery of our ancestors has invigorated our society.\n\nWe are progeny of an ancient civilization, from which we have gleaned valuable knowledge and technological expertise. Our journey through the rift was a momentous one, and will not soon be forgotten.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:146`

## astral_rift.40.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.40.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.40.desc.common$\n\n同时也要感谢先驱者们给我们带来的机遇，因为我们这个文明这是他们求生欲望的见证。我们的成功也是对他们最好的赞颂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:133`

### English

$astral_rift.40.desc.common$\n\nWe should be thankful for the opportunity afforded by our progenitors. Our very civilization is a testament to their will to survive. Our success will be a tribute to them.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:147`

## astral_rift.40.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.40.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.40.desc.common$\n\n我们发现自己的意识是人为工程的产物。我们也是第一次认识到了自己的来处。如今，我们的目标便是超越先祖的简单设计。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:134`

### English

$astral_rift.40.desc.common$\n\nWe have discovered that our consciousness is the product of deliberate engineering. For the first time, we understand how we came to be. Our goal now is to surpass the simple designs of our progenitors.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:148`

## astral_rift.40.desc.unfriendly_nongestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.40.desc.unfriendly_nongestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.40.desc.common$\n\n我们见证了先人的不屈精神，即便被逼上绝境也从不放弃抗争，这重新燃起了我们的征服渴望。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:135`

### English

$astral_rift.40.desc.common$\n\nWe witnessed the indomitable spirit of those who came before us. Backed into a corner, they refused to give up the fight. Our thirst for conquest has been renewed.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:149`

## astral_rift.40.desc.swarm

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.40.desc.swarm&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.40.desc.common$\n\n尽管先祖们最终沦为了猎物，但他们终极猎食者的血液还流淌在我们的身躯中。这种驱使我们的饥渴是他们的遗志，在重新燃起的征服渴望中，我们将吞噬天地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:136`

### English

$astral_rift.40.desc.common$\n\nThough they ultimately ended as prey, our progenitors live on in us as the ultimate predator. The hunger that drives us is a remnant of their will, and we will consume others like them with a renewed thirst for conquest.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:150`

## astral_rift.40.desc.terminators

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.40.desc.terminators&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.40.desc.common$\n\n尽管我们先祖没有我们高效，却将我们设计成了终极的杀戮机器。我们的灭绝倡议是他们的遗志，我们将笃定地执行其中的每一行代码。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:137`

### English

$astral_rift.40.desc.common$\n\nThough they lacked our efficiency, our progenitors designed us to become the ultimate killing machines. Our extermination protocol is a remnant of their will, and we will execute it precisely to the letter.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:151`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.40.a&lang=en)

### 中文

正是如此。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:138`

### English

Indeed.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:153`

## astral_rift.55.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.55.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你是什么人？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:155`

### English

Who are you?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:172`

## astral_rift.56.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.56.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在裂隙后穿行……\n\n无尽的战争……无尽的敌手……你们的先祖应该了然于胸……\n\n你们……位面之子……能够帮助我们……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:156`

### English

we walk behind the rifts...\n\nan endless war... an endless foe... your ancestors know them...\n\nyou... children of planes... can help us...

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:173`

## astral_rift.56.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.56.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们怎么帮？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:157`

### English

How can we help?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:174`

## astral_rift.57.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.57.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

必须同调……\n\n每当你们穿过位面时……我能感受到你们……\n\n很快我们就会找到你们……就会来见你们……\n\n我们相距不远……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:158`

### English

must attune...\n\nwe feel you... every time you breach the planes...\n\nsoon we will know you...  and come to you...\n\nwe are close...

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:175`

## astral_rift.57.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.57.a&lang=en)

### 中文

通讯就这么戛然而止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:159`

### English

The transmission has ended.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:176`

## astral_rift.100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

长廊

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:203`

### English

Corridors

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:227`

## astral_rift.100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现自己身陷于一座前不见头后不见尾的互相联通的舱室网络。\n\n这里的地面是光亮透明的电路板。肉眼可见的能量顺着导体的支路网络流淌着。\n\n这座无尽长廊好像在无限地自我重复，毫无变化。目前的探索一筹莫展，建议另辟蹊径。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:204`

### English

We have found ourselves encased in a network of interconnected chambers with no clear ending or beginning.\n\nThe floor surfaces here are a glassy circuit board. A visible current of energy can be seen flowing through a grid of branching conduits.\n\nThe corridors here seem as if they infinitely repeat themselves with no significant variation. We are making no progress with our current exploration. Making our own path is advised.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:228`

## astral_rift.100.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.100.a&lang=en)

### 中文

打穿地面。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:205`

### English

Break through the floor.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:229`

## astral_rift.100.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.100.b&lang=en)

### 中文

凿穿墙体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:206`

### English

Open the walls.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:230`

## astral_rift.105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不可能的“玻璃”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:207`

### English

Impossible "Glass"

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:232`

## astral_rift.105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们没能打穿地面。它的构成与我们所知的任何透明材料均不相同。暴力无法使其变形，甚至无法在表面留下任何痕迹。\n\n然而，经过一番努力，我们成功的在其中一个管线上用等离子钻打出了一个小小的洞。导通电路的是一种高度浓缩的密织的星界纤维混合物。\n\n和这个发现一样有趣的是，我们也许不可能用这样的方式在地上打开一个通道。我们还得回到正常的探索方式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:208`

### English

Our initial attempts to break through the floor were unsuccessful. Its composition is unlike any known transparent material we have on record. Blunt force will not deform or blemish the surface.\n\nHowever, with a considerable amount of effort, we succeeded in creating a small hole through one of the conduits with a plasma drill. Flowing through the circuitry is a highly concentrated mixture of tightly-woven astral fabric.\n\nAs interesting as this discovery is, it does not seem practical to carve our own passageway through this floor. We will return to exploring in a more traditional manner.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:233`

## astral_rift.110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

再生

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:209`

### English

Regeneration

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:235`

## astral_rift.110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然墙体并不难破坏，但是这种破坏是暂时性的。\n\n无论从墙上移走多少材料，基本建筑材料组成的晶体网络很快就开始自我编织、层层堆叠起来，形成一个与原有的东西非常接近的结构。我们可以看到我们下方的能量通路向着被我们破坏的地方导通着能量。\n\n和这个发现一样有趣的是，我们也许不可能用这样的方式在墙上打开一个通道。我们还得回到正常的探索方式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:210`

### English

The walls here are easily deconstructed, but this is short-lived.\n\nVery soon after any amount of material is removed from the walls, a crystalline network of rudimentary construction materials begins weaving and layering itself on top of one another into a close approximation of what existed before. We can see energy in the conduits below us flow toward the site of each of our deconstructions.\n\nAs interesting as this discovery is, it does not seem practical to carve our own passageway through this floor. We will return to exploring in a more traditional manner.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:236`

## astral_rift.115.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.115.name&lang=en)

### 中文

核心终端

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:211`

### English

The Terminal

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:238`

## astral_rift.115.desc.common.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.115.desc.common.1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们终于在环境中找到了一丝变化：一座巨大的舱室，里面是一台核心终端，操控着布满整个舱室的屏幕。\n\n其中一个屏幕上是周围长廊的地图。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:212`

### English

We have finally found a variation in our surroundings: A large chamber filled with screens controlled by a central terminal.\n\nOn one of the screens is a map of the surrounding corridors.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:239`

## astral_rift.115.desc.common.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.115.desc.common.2&lang=en)

### 中文

如果我们能弄明白控制台，就能大大增进对这里的理解。然而目前也不知道它的代码里面有没有什么安保措施。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:213`

### English

If we can figure out the console controls we could learn far more about this place. However it is unknown what security measures might be present in its code.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:240`

## astral_rift.115.desc.walls

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.115.desc.walls&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.115.desc.common.1$\n\n警报记录记录了我们最近拆墙的位置，还包括了我们拆墙时的监控录像。\n\n$astral_rift.115.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:214`

### English

$astral_rift.115.desc.common.1$\n\nAn alert was logged in the vicinity of our recent wall deconstruction, including video surveillance of ourselves in the act.\n\n$astral_rift.115.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:241`

## astral_rift.115.desc.glass

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.115.desc.glass&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.115.desc.common.1$\n\n警报记录记录了我们最近钻穿地面的位置，还包括了当时的监控录像。\n\n$astral_rift.115.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:215`

### English

$astral_rift.115.desc.common.1$\n\nAn alert was logged in the vicinity of our recent drilling through the floor, including video surveillance of ourselves in the act.\n\n$astral_rift.115.desc.common.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:242`

### 中文

活埋

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:218`

### English

Entombed

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:246`

## astral_rift.120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

破解终端的过程中一个失效保护措施被触发了，这个房间瞬间被一种扩张性的泡沫状物质淹没。还没有人来得及帮上[Leader.GetName]，[Leader.GetHeShe][Leader.GetWasWere]就被完全包裹住了。多次尝试解救[Leader.GetHimHer]也毫无成效，这些材料一经移除又会快速自发归位。\n\n我们按照标准流程拉动紧急锚链，取回了§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!和已收集到的样本。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:219`

### English

In the process of decoding the terminal, a failsafe was triggered and the entire room quickly filled with an expansive foam-like material. Before anyone could assist [Leader.GetName], [Leader.GetHeShe] [Leader.GetWasWere] completely encased. Any attempts to uncover [Leader.GetHimHer] were fruitless, as what material could be removed was instantly replaced.\n\nAs is standard procedure, we have retracted the emergency anchor and recovered the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! and its collected samples.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:247`

## astral_rift.125.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.125.name&lang=en)

### 中文

公式

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:220`

### English

A Formula

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:249`

## astral_rift.125.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.125.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Leader.GetName]能反编译这些代码。这些程序语言高度先进，算法优雅简明。\n\n这整座建筑都是由程序生成的。\n\n尽管我们还没完全破译其模式，但毫无疑问这里的环境是遵从着某种规则。我们取回了一些建材样品，它们呈现出一些自我复制的特性。尽管仍需要进一步测试，我们推测把这种材料放在任意位置并施加正确的引导将能够重建出与这个长廊相似的一整个新体系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:221`

### English

[Leader.GetName] [Leader.GetWasWere] able to decompile the code. The programming language itself is highly advanced and the algorithms have an elegant simplicity.\n\nThis entire structure has been procedurally generated.\n\nThough we have not fully deciphered its patterns, there is very clearly a set of rules this environment is following. We are taking back samples of the building materials which have shown to possess some unique self replicating properties. Further testing is required, but we theorize that placing this material in any location and applying the correct instructions will generate an entirely new system of similar corridors.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:250`

## astral_rift.125.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.125.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是令人着迷的材料。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:222`

### English

What fascinating material.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:251`

## astral_rift.130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们把这台电脑终端拆解成了零件。\n\n在它的线路中找到了数量可观的星界丝缕。\n\n所以这台机器原来到底用来干什么的我们已经不关心了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:224`

### English

We have disassembled the computer terminal for its parts.\n\nA remarkable amount of astral thread was recovered flowing through its circuitry.\n\nWhatever purpose this machine had is no longer our concern.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:254`

## astral_rift.140.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.140.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetLeaderName]竟然从星界裂隙中回来了。\n\n[Root.Leader.GetHeSheCap]……发生了一些变化。[Root.Leader.GetHisHer]似乎被囚禁在了这个时间线和其他时间线之间，年龄变得无法测量。\n\n同样令人难以理解的是，[Root.Leader.GetHeShe]好像也获得了一些计算研究的天赋。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:227`

### English

[Root.GetLeaderName] [Root.GetHasHave] unexpectedly returned from [Root.Leader.GetHisHer] journey inside one of the Astral Rifts.\n\n[Root.Leader.GetHeSheCap] [Root.Leader.IsAre]... changed. Caught somewhere between this time and another, [Root.Leader.GetHisHer] age seems indeterminable.\n\nInexplicably, [Root.Leader.GetHeShe] also seemed to have gained an affinity for calculative research.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:258`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.200.name&lang=en)

### 中文

远古之战

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:232`

### English

An Ancient Battle

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:265`

## astral_rift.200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个裂隙中包含着一次史诗之战的余波。\n\n残留的只有无数的军舰残骸和硕大无朋的生物尸体，比我们最大战舰的全长还要大上十倍。\n\n与这些战舰残破的船体形成鲜明对比的是，尸体的状态非常好。不知这些舰船使用的是什么武器，但似乎几乎没有造成损坏。我们尚未确定该生物的死因，但似乎不太可能是战斗的结果。\n\n这只野兽一定有非凡的耐力。分析它的分子组成有助于我们加固舰队的船体。另一方面，我们也可以尝试研究它的内部，也许可以了解这种生物是如何死亡的，以及这里到底发生了什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:233`

### English

This rift contains the aftermath of a titanic struggle.\n\nAll that is left is the wreckage of a military fleet and the corpse of a monstrous creature, ten times the largest of the battleships from tip to tail.\n\nIn contrast to the mangled hulls of the warships, the carcass is in remarkably good shape. Whatever weapons these ships used, they seem to have hardly damaged it. We have not yet determined what caused this creature's demise, but it seems unlikely it was the result of the battle.\n\nThe beast must have had remarkable durability. Analyzing its molecular composition should help us strengthen the hulls of our fleet. On the other hand, we could attempt to study its internals, perhaps learning how this creature died, and what exactly occurred here.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:266`

## astral_rift.200.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.200.a&lang=en)

### 中文

研究组成

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:234`

### English

Study its composition.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:267`

### English

Let's get in there.

## astral_rift.205.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.205.name&lang=en)

### 中文

异星装甲

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:236`

### English

Exotic Armor

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:270`

## astral_rift.205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这只巨兽的装甲所使用的材料组成出乎我们的意料。\n\n他的鳞片具有一种复杂的有机金属晶格互锁结构，使其能够抵御动能冲击。这简直是大自然的鬼斧神工。\n\n对其背后原理的理解促进我们的工程师们重新思考构建太空舰船船体的方式。\n\n如果在战场上碰见那可真是一个可怕的对手。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:237`

### English

The material composition of the leviathan's armor is beyond our expectations.\n\nIts scales contain a mix of organic and metallic components locked together in a complex lattice that reinforces itself against kinetic shock. It is a true marvel of nature.\n\nUnderstanding the principles behind it have inspired our engineers to rethink how we construct the hulls of our own spaceships.\n\nThis must have been a terrifying adversary to meet in battle.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:271`

## astral_rift.205.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.205.a&lang=en)

### English

## astral_rift.210.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.210.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不明躯体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:239`

### English

Uncharted Body

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:274`

## astral_rift.210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们进入了那局漂浮的尸体内部。\n\n因为无法钻穿这只生物令人惊异的装甲，在大量爆炸物的帮助下我们撬开了它的口部。一支小队通过这只巨兽的口进入，开始探索它的内部结构，收集组织样本。\n\n其内部令人惊讶。无数排利齿表明这是一种猎食性生物。非常高效的消化系统占据了它躯体的大部分空间，并且表明这只生物生来可以消化各种物质。\n\n我们也能以此估计它的死亡时间和原因：尽管食性复杂，但它显然发生了脱水。这也表明它可能并不是来自这个维度，它在这里找不到可以饮用的物质。\n\n根据同位素定年，它的躯体已经死亡超过五万年。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:240`

### English

We have entered the derelict carcass.\n\nWhile we were unable to penetrate the remarkable armor of the creature, we did succeed in opening its mouth with the aid of a large quantity of explosives. A small team ventured through the mouth of the beast to better understand its internals and collect samples of its tissues.\n\nThe interior is fascinating. Countless rows of sharp teeth indicate that this was a predatory organism. A very efficient digestive system occupied the majority of its bulk, and revealed that this creature was in the habit of consuming a wide variety of materials.\n\nWe were also able to estimate the time and cause of death: despite the creature's omnivorous habits, there were clear signs of dehydration. This could indicate that it is not native to this dimension, finding nothing here to drink.\n\nBased on carbon-dating, this organism passed away over fifty thousand years ago.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:275`

## astral_rift.215.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.215.name&lang=en)

### 中文

一十二面

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:243`

### English

A Dozen Faces

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:279`

## astral_rift.215.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.215.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们研究了这个裂隙中的战舰残骸。\n\n这种外形舰船光滑的表面与我们的§Y$tech_rift_sphere_plural$§!设计颇有相似之处。值得一提的是，似乎这些舰船是设计用于在不依靠锚链撤退而自由进出裂隙的。\n\n我们的工程师还在努力解析这些科技，其他的团队则在最大的残骸内部发现了一个大型的十二面体物体。不进行进一步的测试我们无从猜测其用途，但它储存的谨慎程度毫无疑问暗示了其重要性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:244`

### English

We've examined the remains of the warships within this rift.\n\nThe smooth surfaces of the alien crafts bear similarities to the design of our §Y$tech_rift_sphere_plural$§!. Remarkably, it seems that these vessels were designed to freely enter and exit rifts without the need of anchor cable extraction.\n\nWhile our engineers worked on understanding this technology, the rest of the team discovered a large dodecahedron nestled in the interior of the largest wreck. Without further testing it is impossible to speculate on its purpose, but its careful storage indicates it would have been regarded as important.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:280`

## astral_rift.215.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.215.a&lang=en)

### English

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:281`

激活

### English

Activation

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:284`

## astral_rift.220.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.220.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的团队将这个十二面体带到了§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!上进行进一步测试。\n\n对它发现地的进一步调查发现了痕量的星界丝缕。将星界丝缕注入十二面体后，该物体会开始以极高的振动，甚至外形都逐渐模糊成为一个球体。\n\n装置激活后，我们来时的裂隙被封闭了。而当装置关闭时，裂隙又再度打开。\n\n基于这一实验，我们推断，某个远古帝国在面对一个无法匹敌的对手时，他们用这个科技最后一搏，牺牲了他们的舰队把这只生物封禁于此。\n\n这件令人惊异的装置可以让我们大受裨益。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:248`

### English

Our team brought the dodecahedron aboard the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! for further examination.\n\nA reinvestigation of its original housing found trace amounts of astral thread. Feeding some into the dodecahedron caused the object to vibrate at a frequency so high that its blurred form began to resemble a sphere.\n\nWhile the device was active, the rift we entered through was sealed. As soon as the device was 'switched off', it reappeared.\n\nBased on this experiment, we can suppose that faced with a foe they could not destroy, some ancient empire used this technology in a desperate attempt to lock the creature away, sacrificing their fleet in the process.\n\nThis remarkable device could prove a significant boon for us.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:285`

## astral_rift.225.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.225.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这件十二面体可是稀有材料的宝库。其内部密布着$astral_threads$，其图案表明它构成了复杂的电路网络。装置的核心则储存着大量的暗物质。这件文物是如何运作的还是一个谜，但我们可以充分地利用构成它的材料。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:251`

### English

The dodecahedron was a treasure trove of rare materials. Its internals were dense with $astral_threads$ in patterns that suggested a complicated network of circuitry. The central core housed a significant quantity of Dark Matter. How this artifact worked remains a mystery, but we can certainly make use of its base components.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:289`

## decision_fractal_seed_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adecision_fractal_seed_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些从星界裂隙另一侧取回的透明球体可以将自身弯曲入额外维度，增加周围空间的有效容积。我们应当慎重地安置它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:258`

### English

These transparent globes recovered from beyond an Astral Rift are able to bend themselves into an extra dimension, increasing the effective size of their surrounding space. We should place them carefully.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:298`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_fractal_seed_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$decision_fractal_seed_desc$

### English

$decision_fractal_seed_desc$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:300`

## astral_rift.300.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.300.name&lang=en)

### 中文

怪异几何

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:261`

### English

Strange Geometry

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:302`

## astral_rift.300.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.300.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们进入了一个充满令人困惑的视觉奇景、几何图案和变幻莫测的色彩的空间。\n\n尽管周围的环境看起来并不危险，但它们就像视错觉一样让人迷失方向。构成这个宇宙的物质令人困惑。无论这些样本如何放大，我们都会看到同样的重复模式，如同自身的无限浓缩。\n\n很明显，在这个裂隙中有一些我们还不了解的物理原理。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:262`

### English

We have entered a space of confusing visual spectacles, geometric patterns, and shifting colors.\n\nThe surroundings do not appear dangerous, but they are as disorientating as an optical illusion. Attempts to analyze the material that makes up this universe are baffling. No matter how intensely the samples are magnified, we are confronted by the same repeating patterns. It is as if they condense towards infinity.\n\nClearly, there are physical principles operating inside of this rift that we do not yet understand.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:303`

## astral_rift.300.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.300.a&lang=en)

### 中文

进一步探索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:263`

### English

Explore further.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:304`

## astral_rift.300.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.300.b&lang=en)

### 中文

在原地研究环境。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:264`

### English

Stay put. Study the surroundings.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:305`

## astral_rift.305.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.305.name&lang=en)

### 中文

多维度图样

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:265`

### English

Multi-Dimensional Patterns

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:307`

## astral_rift.305.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.305.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这里的空间与我们的认知大相径庭。\n\n我们舰船的移动不能带来预期的结果。向前推进时，舰船会向左平移；增大推力时反而将减慢速度。\n\n[Leader.GetName]推测，这个宇宙具有超越三个以上的标准维度，这也带来了一些突破。也许以这个眼光检测周围环境，我们就可以开始理解这些图样。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:266`

### English

Space works differently here.\n\nThe movements of our ship do not correspond to expected results. Propelling forward, we shift left; increasing our thrust, we slow down.\n\nA breakthrough was made when [Leader.GetName] theorized that this universe allows access to more than the standard three spatial dimensions. Perhaps by examining our surroundings through this lens we can begin to find meaning in these patterns.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:308`

## astral_rift.305.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.305.a&lang=en)

### 中文

继续调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:267`

### English

Continue your investigations.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:309`

## astral_rift.310.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.310.name&lang=en)

### 中文

多维度分析

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:268`

### English

Multi-Dimensional Analysis

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:311`

## astral_rift.310.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.310.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

如果不能充分理解这个宇宙，在其中我们寸步难行。因此，我们的所有子个体都被分派研究周围的环境。\n起先这些数据似乎是毫无关联的随机噪音，直到[Leader.GetName]取得了突破：令人惊讶的是，这个宇宙具有超过三个的标准维度。也许以这个眼光检测周围环境，我们就可以开始理解这些图样。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:269`

### English

It would be foolhardy to travel through this universe without understanding it more completely. Instead, every one of our drones has been dispatched in an effort to analyze our surroundings.\nAt first the data seemed to be an incoherent mess of random noise, until a breakthrough was made by [Leader.GetName]: incredibly, this universe provides access to more than the standard three spatial dimensions. Perhaps by examining things through this lens we can begin to find meaning in the patterns.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:312`

## astral_rift.310.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.310.a&lang=en)

### 中文

进一步研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:270`

### English

Study further.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:313`

## astral_rift.315.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.315.name&lang=en)

### 中文

瓶中信

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:271`

### English

Message in a Bottle

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:315`

## astral_rift.315.desc.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.315.desc.common&lang=en)

### 中文

对我们周围环境图样进行四维空间外推，我们成功的创建了一副可用的地图。这揭示了这些重复图样中存在着一个“中央舱室”。\n\n从四维空间的角度来看，我们可以沿一条直线到达裂隙的中央，但我们自己却感觉通过了一条扭曲的轨迹、穿过一座难以理解的迷宫。要穿过这个迷宫，我们需要急转弯，有时还得往回走，但我们最终还是到达了目的地。\n\n这个新空间非常巨大，大到逻辑上无法包含在舱室中，必然是更多折叠维度的结果。\n\n在它的中心，我们找到了一个熟悉的装置。上面书写这的是我们自己的语言，是一套如何进一步进行的说明。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:272`

### English

By extrapolating the patterns of our surroundings in four spatial dimensions, we've succeeded in creating a useable map. This has revealed the existence of a 'central chamber' within the repeated patterns.\n\nWhile from the perspective of four dimensions our travel to the middle of the rift was a straight line, our own experience was a twisting trajectory through an incomprehensible labyrinth. Sharp turns and the occasional backtrack were required to traverse this maze, but we eventually reached our goal.\n\nThis new space is vast; it is far too large to logically be contained within the chamber itself, and must be the result of even more folded dimensions.\n\nAt its very center, we recovered a familiar device. Written in our own language, it was a set of instructions on how to proceed further.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:316`

## astral_rift.315.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.315.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.315.desc.common$\n\n而他上面的签名正式[Leader.GetName]。这是一封来自未来自己的信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:273`

### English

$astral_rift.315.desc.common$\n\nIt was signed by none other than [Leader.GetName]. This is a message from us... in the future.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:317`

### English

$astral_rift.315.desc.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:318`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.315.a&lang=en)

这可信吗？

### English

## astral_rift.320.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.320.name&lang=en)

### 中文

时间旅行

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:276`

### English

Time Traveling

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:321`

## astral_rift.320.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.320.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在所有的时间轴上进行旅行。\n\n按照摆在我们面前的指示，我们轻松地穿越了未知数量的超空间维度。好几回，我们看到自己从另一条路线穿过。我们的其他一些副本似乎处于困境中，他们§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!的腐蚀破旧也说明他们在这个空间迷失了许久。\n\n幸运的是，到目前为止我们还没有直接遭遇。我们已经为自己留下了指示，以防止这种危险的时间同步发生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:277`

### English

We are traveling through time across all axes.\n\nFollowing the instructions laid before us, we navigated easily through an unknown amount of super-spatial dimensions. At various points, we witnessed ourselves crossing from another course. Some of our other copies appeared to be in distress, with the wear and corrosion on their §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! indicating they have been lost in this space for much too long.\n\nLuckily, we have avoided direct encounters thus far. We have left our own instructions behind us to prevent such a dangerous temporal simultaneity from occurring.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:322`

## astral_rift.320.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.320.a&lang=en)

### 中文

令人不安……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:278`

### English

Unsettling...

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:323`

## astral_rift.325.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.325.name&lang=en)

### 中文

分形之种

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:279`

### English

Fractal Seeds

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:325`

## astral_rift.325.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.325.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

终于，我们达到了或许就是这个迷宫真正终点的中心。\n\n我们处在一个由许多未知维度编织成的空间，我们的传感器对其的显示是如同万花筒一般的跃动色彩。在其最核心处漂浮着三个透明球仪，每一个都包含着向一个轴向无限重复的图样。我推测，这个宇宙所包含的每一个维度都有一个球仪与之对应，但我们只能与我们可观测的三个发生交互。\n\n这里就再也没有什么指引了。缺失来自未来自我的信息可能表明，与这些球仪互动可能会产生可怕的后果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:280`

### English

At last we have reached the center of what must be the true end of this maze.\n\nWe are in a space composed of so many unknown dimensions that our senses can only perceive it as a wild kaleidoscope of colors and motion. At its very core, three transparent globes are floating, each containing an infinitely repeating pattern in one axial direction. We suspect that there must be a globe for each dimension contained in this universe, but we can only interact with our perceivable three.\n\nThere are no further instructions to be found. This lack of messages from our future selves could indicate that interacting with these globes may have dire consequences.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:326`

## astral_rift.325.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.325.a&lang=en)

### 中文

取回球仪。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:281`

### English

Retrieve the globes.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:327`

## astral_rift.325.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.325.b&lang=en)

我们到此为止吧。

### English

We are done here.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:328`

## astral_rift.330.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.330.name&lang=en)

### 中文

维度容器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:283`

### English

Dimensional Containers

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:330`

## astral_rift.330.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.330.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们从裂隙回来时，有另两个球体也跟随着我们。也许是由其他维度的我们驾驶的，我们“仨”在到达阈限时融合（或者说坍缩？）成了一体。\n\n怪异的是，我们取回的这三个球仪并没有像这样也合并在一起。同一个物体的九个时间版本出现在了同一个空间。\n\n当放置于独立的容器中，它们自发地自我弯曲进入额外维度，增加了周围空间的有效容积。我们还不确定这个效应的扩展极限，但推测这个装置可以在人口稠密的区域派上大用场。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:284`

### English

As we returned back through the rift, two other Spheres came with us. Presumably crewed by other versions of ourselves, we 'three' fused (or were reduced?) into one at its threshold.\n\nStrangely, the three orbs we recovered did not converge in the same manner. Nine temporal versions of the same objects now exist in one space.\n\nWhen placed in individual containers, they naturally bend themselves into an extra dimension and increase the effective size of their surrounding space. We are not sure yet of the limits of this expansion, but theorize these devices could be of great use in densely populated areas.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:331`

## astral_rift.330.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.330.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真方便啊。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:285`

### English

How convenient.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:332`

## fractal_seeds_available_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afractal_seeds_available_tt&lang=en)

### 中文

你将拥有[Explorer.num_authorized_fractal_seeds]个§Y分形之种§!，可以通过§H殖民地决议§!放置在某一处殖民地上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:286`

### English

You will have [Explorer.num_authorized_fractal_seeds] §YFractal Seeds§! to place on colonies, via §HColony Decisions§!.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:333`

## astral_rift.335.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.335.name&lang=en)

### 中文

亲密接触

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:287`

### English

Close Encounters

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:335`

## astral_rift.335.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.335.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们选择不将球仪带走，最终我们还是发现它们三个出现在裂隙的这端。一定是另一个我们取回了它们。\n\n通过实验，我们发现了这些球仪具有有趣又实用的性质。具体来说，它们可以弯曲进入额外维度，增加周围空间的有效容积。我们还不确定这个效应的扩展极限，但推测这个装置可以在人口稠密的区域派上大用场。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:288`

### English

Though we chose not to take any of the globes with us, we have nonetheless ended up with all three of them on the near side of the rift. One of our other selves must have recovered them.\n\nOn experimentation, they exhibit interesting and useful properties. Specifically, they bend into an extra dimension, increasing the effective size of their surrounding space. We are not sure yet of the limits of this expansion, but theorize these devices could be of great use in densely populated areas.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:336`

## astral_rift.335.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.335.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们帮了自己一把。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:289`

### English

We've helped ourselves.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:337`

### English

Subnautical

## astral_rift.500.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.500.name&lang=en)

### 中文

下潜

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:294`

### English

Submerged

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:344`

## astral_rift.500.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.500.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

压力的变化既突然又剧烈；球体抵达的地方似乎是浩瀚海洋的深处。这里一片漆黑，我们的仪器没有探测到任何生命、漂浮沉积物或颗粒物质的迹象。\n\n根据水压判断，我们在表面以下极深处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:295`

### English

The change in pressure was as sudden as it was violent; the Sphere arrived in the depths of what appears to be a vast ocean. All is dark. Our instruments show no signs of life, floating sediment, or particulate matter.\n\nJudging by the high water pressure, we are somewhere deep below the surface.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:345`

## astral_rift.500.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.500.a&lang=en)

### 中文

继续下潜。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:296`

### English

Dive deeper.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:346`

## astral_rift.500.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.500.b&lang=en)

### 中文

向海面上浮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:297`

### English

Head to the surface.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:347`

## astral_rift.505.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.505.name&lang=en)

### 中文

意外结果

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:298`

### English

Unexpected Results

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:349`

## astral_rift.505.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.505.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

好像出了什么问题。\n\n我们在水中穿行了数个小时，但周围的环境一成不变。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:299`

### English

Something is wrong.\n\nWe have been traveling through the water for hours, but have seen no change in our surroundings.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:350`

## astral_rift.505.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.505.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.505.desc$\n\n舵手认为，可能有一种作用在我们身上的力让引擎无法客服。他建议我们试试短距离跃迁。或者自行离开球体，把自己附着在低密度的充气式物体上，这样就有可能会浮到表面。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:300`

### English

$astral_rift.505.desc$\n\nThe helmsman believes there is some force acting on us that our engines cannot overcome. He recommends we try a short jump. Alternatively, if we exit the Sphere and attach ourselves to low density inflatables we may simply float to the surface.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:351`

## astral_rift.505.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.505.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.505.desc$\n\n似乎有一种我们引擎无法克服的力整影响着我们。\n\n我们也许可以通过短距跃迁的方式克服。或者我们可以离开球体，把自己附着在低密度的充气式物体上，这样就有可能会浮到表面。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:301`

### English

$astral_rift.505.desc$\n\nThere is some force acting on us that our engines cannot overcome.\n\nWe may be able to overpower it with a short jump. Alternatively, if we exit the Sphere and attach ourselves to low density inflatables we may simply float to the surface.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:352`

## astral_rift.505.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.505.a&lang=en)

引擎启动。

### English
