## astral_rift.505.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.505.b&lang=en)

### 中文

穿好抗压服，我们游上去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:303`

### English

Don pressure suits. We swim.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:354`

## astral_rift.510.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.510.name&lang=en)

### 中文

行星尺寸泡泡

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:304`

### English

A Planet-Sized Bubble

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:356`

## astral_rift.510.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.510.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

短距跃迁生效了。我们出现在了靠近表面的区域。在突破水面后，我们发现我们事实上处于一个真空宇宙中行星大小的空泡中。\n\n我们之前的速度完全无法脱离其引力束缚。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:305`

### English

The short jump seems to have worked. We have appeared near the surface. After breaking through, we have discovered that we were in fact inside of a planet-sized bubble in the vacuum of space.\n\nOur previous speeds were not enough to break through its gravitational forces.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:357`

## astral_rift.515.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.515.name&lang=en)

### 中文

自游

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:306`

### English

We Swim

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:359`

## astral_rift.515.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.515.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们部署了超长缆线，通过特殊的气闸离开了球体。\n\n我们都将紧急浮标充气后，低密度将我们以惊人的速度带到了海面。\n\n一路上我们的抗压服过滤器收集到了非常高浓度的星界纤维丝线。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:307`

### English

Remote tethers were deployed, and we have exited the Sphere through the specialized airlock.\n\nWe each inflated our emergency buoys as briefed and, with surprising speed, our lower densities raised us to the surface.\n\nAlong the way, the filters on our suit gathered some very highly concentrated threads of astral fabric.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:360`

## astral_rift.515.desc.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.515.desc.2&lang=en)

### 中文

当我们穿着笨重的抗压服刺破水面时，发现其实我们身处一个真空宇宙中行星大小的泡泡里。\n\n§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!的密度过高无法逃脱这里的引力。\n\n我们向球体发回了信号，遥控其短距跃迁到我们的位置附近。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:308`

### English

After our bulky suits pierced through the surface, we discovered that we were in fact inside of a planet-sized bubble in the vacuum of space.\n\nThe §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!'s density was too high to overcome the gravitational forces present here.\n\nWe have radioed back to the Sphere and ordered a short jump near our location.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:361`

## astral_rift.515.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.515.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.515.desc.1$\n\n$astral_rift.515.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:309`

### English

$astral_rift.515.desc.1$\n\n$astral_rift.515.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:362`

## astral_rift.515.desc.aquatic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.515.desc.aquatic&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.515.desc.1$\n\n不过至少，这泳游得我们身心畅快。我们很高兴有这种回归天然环境的机会。\n\n$astral_rift.515.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:310`

### English

$astral_rift.515.desc.1$\n\nIt was a pleasurable swim, to say the least. We relished the opportunity to return to our natural environment.\n\n$astral_rift.515.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:363`

## astral_rift.520.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.520.name&lang=en)

### 中文

泡泡行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:311`

### English

Planetoid Bubbles

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:365`

## astral_rift.520.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.520.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们周围的空间看起来像是一瓶恒星大小的液体在低重力下爆炸，行星大小的微晶液体气泡在四周漂浮。附近有一颗恒星，我们正无力地围绕着它运动。它看起来非常古老、暗淡而冰冷。\n\n而此处还有别的东西。虽说不是一颗恒星，却有恒星般大小。它是由无数互相交织的碎片组成，就像一个无比巨大的3D拼图。扫描显示，它是由许多珍贵的金属和材料构成的，这些物质和它所围绕的恒星中所蕴含的物质完全一致。很有可能其中一者诞生自另一者之中，或是具有相同的起源。\n\n仔细观察会发现，那些碎片正在缓慢浮动。偶尔会有一个新的巨大液体泡泡从这座构造中泄出。\n\n如果它有生命，那看起来还没有注意到我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:312`

### English

The space around us looks as if a star-sized bottle of liquid exploded in low gravity. Planetoid-sized bubbles of crystalline liquid float all around us. There is a nearby star which we are weakly orbiting. It looks old, dark, and cold.\n\nThere is something else. It is not a star, but just as big. It is constructed of massive interconnected fragments, like a 3-dimensional puzzle on a gigantic scale. Scans determine it to be composed of various precious metals and minerals that are nearly identical to that of what is inside of the star it orbits. It is likely one formed from the other, or from a common source.\n\nOn closer inspection, the pieces are slowly moving and shifting. Occasionally, a large bubble of new liquid pours out from its architecture.\n\nIf it is alive, it has not seemed to notice us.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:366`

### English

## astral_rift.525.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.525.name&lang=en)

### 中文

通讯

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:315`

### English

Communication

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:370`

## astral_rift.525.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.525.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

如此巨大的构造存在却不需要任何管理员和维护，它必然拥有某种程度的自主性。\n\n我们扫描了所有的音频频段但一无所获。\n\n它只会释放持续不断令人恶寒的哀嚎。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:316`

### English

For such a massive mechanism to exist without caretakers or maintenance it must have some form of autonomy.\n\nWe cycled through all available audio frequencies, then found something.\n\nIt is emitting a constant, terrible wailing.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:371`

## astral_rift.525.desc.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.525.desc.2&lang=en)

### 中文

我们目前准备尝试用所有已知的语言在该频段下发出广播以期进行通讯。\n\n我们该说些什么呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:317`

### English

We are currently prepared to broadcast a message in every known language tuned to this frequency in an attempt to communicate.\n\nWhat should we say?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:372`

## astral_rift.525.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.525.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.525.desc.1$\n\n要不是舵手默默地切断了这个信号，恐怕船员们的精神很快就濒临崩溃了。\n\n$astral_rift.525.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:318`

### English

$astral_rift.525.desc.1$\n\nThe crew's senses quickly became overwhelmed until the helmsman cut the feed without a word.\n\n$astral_rift.525.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:373`

### English

## astral_rift.530.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.530.name&lang=en)

### 中文

靠近

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:323`

### English

The Approach

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:379`

## astral_rift.530.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.530.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们慢慢地靠近，小心地避开周围的水泡。\n\n这个物体一直在运动。它的各个部件互相摩擦所产生的热量足以熔化大多数已知的金属。据我们的理解，它的核心是完全熔化的状态。\n\n我们正在向计算机上传它的结构细节以进行进一步研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:324`

### English

We approached slowly, carefully navigating around nearby bubbles of water.\n\n The object is in constant motion. The heat generated by the friction of the interwoven pieces is enough to melt most known metals. Its core, from what we can tell, is completely molten.\n\nWe are uploading its structural details to our computers for further study.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:380`

## astral_rift.530.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.530.a&lang=en)

### 中文

再靠近点儿……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:325`

### English

Get closer...

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:381`

## astral_rift.535.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.535.name&lang=en)

### 中文

苏醒

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:326`

### English

Awakening

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:383`

## astral_rift.535.answer.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.535.answer.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这个物体突然……醒了。它的碎片被向外拉开，以难以置信的速度开始旋转。它的重力井也陡然骤增，我们以极高的加速度被拉了过去。\n\n一颗如同小型恒星一般的火球从它的碎片之间涌现。这个熔炉只出现在物体的其中一侧，它缓缓转向我们，向我们释放出狂暴的炽热。\n\n一股电磁辐射脉冲突然爆发，让我们的船颤抖起来。控制台上的每个警报都响了起来，或者干脆发出了故障信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:327`

### English

The object has suddenly... awoken. Its pieces have pulled outward and are rotating at an incredible speed. Its gravity well has dramatically increased, and we have been pulled forward at high acceleration.\n\nA fiery sphere, like a small star, has emerged from within its pieces. This furnace, present on only one side of the object, has spun slowly toward us and is emitting a violent heat in our direction.\n\nA pulse of electromagnetic radiation then burst forth, and our vessel shuddered. Every alarm on the console activated, or signaled in error.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:384`

## astral_rift.535.answer.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.535.answer.b&lang=en)

### 中文

这个物体突然……醒了。它的碎片被向外拉开，以难以置信的速度开始旋转。它的重力井也陡然骤增，我们以极高的加速度被拉了过去。\n\n一颗如同小型恒星一般的火球从它的碎片之间涌现。这个熔炉只出现在物体的其中一侧，它缓缓转向我们，向我们释放出狂暴的炽热。\n\n一股电磁辐射脉冲突然爆发，让我们的船颤抖起来。控制台上的每个警报都响了起来，或者干脆发出了故障信号——而翻译功能却维持正常。\n\n我们收到了一个用二进制编码写成的，许多我们已知语言短语：§E“悲痛。走开。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:328`

### English

The object has suddenly... awoken. Its pieces have pulled outward and are rotating at an incredible speed. Its gravity well has dramatically increased, and we have been pulled forward at high acceleration.\n\nA fiery sphere, like a small star, has emerged from within its pieces. This furnace, present on only one side of the object, has spun slowly toward us and is emitting a violent heat in our direction.\n\nA pulse of electromagnetic radiation then burst forth, and our vessel shuddered. Every alarm on the console activated, or signaled in error - except for the translator.\n\nA phrase was registered in several of our known languages, written in binary code: §E"Sorrow. Leave us."§!

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:385`

## astral_rift.535.answer.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.535.answer.c&lang=en)

### 中文

这个物体突然……醒了。它的碎片被向外拉开，以难以置信的速度开始旋转。它的重力井也陡然骤增，我们以极高的加速度被拉了过去。\n\n一颗如同小型恒星一般的火球从它的碎片之间涌现。这个熔炉只出现在物体的其中一侧，它缓缓转向我们，向我们释放出狂暴的炽热。\n\n一股电磁辐射脉冲突然爆发，让我们的船颤抖起来。控制台上的每个警报都响了起来，或者干脆发出了故障信号——而翻译功能却维持正常。\n\n我们收到了一个用二进制编码写成的，许多我们已知语言短语：§E“绝望。我哀嚎。你们走。马上。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:329`

### English

The object has suddenly... awoken. Its pieces have pulled outward and are rotating at an incredible speed. Its gravity well has dramatically increased, and we have been pulled forward at high acceleration.\n\nA fiery sphere, like a small star, has emerged from within its pieces. This furnace, present on only one side of the object, has spun slowly toward us and is emitting a violent heat in our direction.\n\nA pulse of electromagnetic radiation then burst forth, and our vessel shuddered. Every alarm on the console activated, or signaled in error - except for the translator.\n\nA phrase was registered in several of our known languages, written in binary code: §E"No hope. I mourn. You leave. Now."§!

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:386`

## astral_rift.535.desc.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.535.desc.2&lang=en)

### 中文

船体到达了材料极限。\n\n我们赶紧拉动紧急锚链，尽可能取回了样板。\n\n不管它到底是什么，似乎这超出了我们所能干涉的范畴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:330`

### English

Our vessel has reached its material limit.\n\nWe have pulled the emergency anchor, and are returning with what specimens we can.\n\nWhatever it is, it seems beyond our abilities to interfere.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:387`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atower_rift_leader_desc&lang=en)

### English

§Y[Root.GetName]§! was recovered from a great tower inside of an $ASTRAL_RIFT$, where [Root.GetSheHe] previously held a position as the leader of a doomsday cult.

巨塔

### English

## astral_rift.600.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.600.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

到达不久后，我们着陆在一个岩石基座上，它的尺寸恰好与§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!相合。事实上，这个位置与我们的需求吻合到说是巧合也未免太勉强了。\n\n一片广阔而灿烂的天空在我们的四面八方伸展开去。初步扫描显示，我们正坐在一座巨大的塔顶，塔底被涌动的云层挡住了视线。\n\n在底座的边缘有一位孤独的生物。它穿着一件破烂的、满是灰尘的长袍，显然在等着迎接我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:340`

### English

Soon after arrival, we touched down on a stone pedestal that conforms perfectly to the dimensions of the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!. In fact, the location is so obviously suited to our needs that the idea of it being a coincidence has been rejected out of hand.\n\nIn all directions, a vast and radiant sky stretches before us. Initial scans indicate we are sitting atop a massive tower, the bottom of which is blocked from view by the swirling layers of cloud.\n\nAt the edge of the pedestal is a solitary creature. It wears a tattered, dusty robe, and is apparently waiting to greet us.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:400`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.600.a&lang=en)

### English

## astral_rift.605.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.605.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于无法翻译这个生物的语言，我们干脆用一种更直接的方式进行沟通，间断地闪亮§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!的外部灯光。\n\n这个生物一下子跪到了地上。我们一开始还以为是我们伤到了他，但很快发现他是在表达某种赞美或者恳求。\n\n不久之后，一群穿着相似的生物围到了基座边上，他们手中拿着一个篮子，其中装的东西我们大概只能推测是礼物之类的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:345`

### English

With no means of fully translating the creature's language, we resorted to a more direct form of communication, intermittently flashing the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!'s external lights.\n\nWithin moments, the creature fell to the ground. At first we were concerned that we had harmed it, but it soon became clear that it was simply offering some sort of praise or supplication.\n\nNot long after, a coterie of similarly dressed beings approached the pedestal, each bearing a basket of what we can only assume to be gifts.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:406`

## astral_rift.610.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.610.name&lang=en)

### 中文

恭迎已久

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:349`

### English

A Long Awaited Welcome

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:411`

## astral_rift.610.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.610.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们安全地待在球体内，花费了一些时间调查这个地方。这里离地的高度大约是2500米，对于这种科技发展水平的社会来说，要建造如此高度的摩天巨塔显然要花费难以想象的努力。事实上，我们甚至正在见证这个文明首次进入太空的尝试。更奇怪的是，这个岩石基座上雕刻着一些似乎与在§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!内找到的相同的图案。\n\n这个生物骄傲地站在我们面前，张开双臂。这显然是一个欢乐的时刻。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:350`

### English

From the safety of the Sphere, we spent some time investigating the site. Our altitude is estimated at around 2,500 meters. It must have been a great effort for a society at this level of technological development to construct a spire at such a height. In fact, we may be looking at this civilization's first effort at reaching space. Even more intriguingly, the stone pedestal is carved with markings and illustrations that look nearly identical to those found inside the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!.\n\nThe creature stands proudly before us, its arms stretched wide. This is clearly a joyous moment.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:412`

## astral_rift.611.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.611.name&lang=en)

### 中文

一网打尽

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:355`

### English

Fish in a Barrel

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:418`

## astral_rift.611.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.611.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

裂隙球从仪式巨塔高高升起，这一定让下面的生物产生了某种具有精神意义的时刻。不幸的是，紧接着我们的飞船以极限速度坠落，摧毁了基座附近的所有生命形式。他们默默地站着，举起手臂，直到最后一刻。\n\n现在我们可以继续不受任何干扰探索这个环境了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:356`

### English

The raising of the Rift Sphere high above the ceremonial tower must have inspired a moment of spiritual significance for the beings below. Unfortunately for them, it was followed quickly by the drop of our vessel at terminal velocity, obliterating all lifeforms near the pedestal. They stood silently, arms raised, until the very end.\n\nNow we may continue to explore this environment without further disturbance.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:419`

## astral_rift.611.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.611.a&lang=en)

### 中文

扫描星球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:357`

### English

Scan the planet.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:420`

## astral_rift.615.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.615.name&lang=en)

### 中文

东道主

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:358`

### English

Playing Host

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:422`

## astral_rift.615.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.615.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们打开了舱门，这个生物犹犹豫豫地走了进来。\n\n他每迈出一步，步伐就变得自信了一些，但仍然可以看出他浑身每一寸都高度紧绷着。谁知道他到底等待这一刻的到来有多长时间了。\n\n我们不确定他如何知道我们会到来，但我们确定把它带上来应该不会有什么问题。他对着我们船上的仪器瞠目结舌，很快就惊得昏阙在了甲板地上。\n\n在对他藏在袍子里的文件进行了一些修复和研究之后，我们得出了这样的结论：这个特殊的生物是同类中具有一定影响力的领袖。\n\n我们认为，通过一些整合，他们将对我们自己的社会有很大的好处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:359`

### English

We opened our vessel, and the creature tentatively approached.\n\nWith each step it gained more confidence, but we could tell its vital systems were highly elevated. How long has it waited for this moment to take place?\n\nWe are not sure how it knew we would be coming, but we are sure it is safe to bring aboard. It gasped in awe at the instruments of our vessel. Overcome with excitement, it quickly passed out onto the deck plating.\n\nAfter some rehabilitation and study of the documents it had rolled up inside of its robes, we were able to reach the conclusion that this particular creature is an influential leader among its peers.\n\nWe think, with some integration, they would be a great benefit to our own society.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:423`

## astral_rift.615.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.615.a&lang=en)

邀请他来我们的帝国。

### English

Invite them to our empire.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:424`

### English

A §YScientist§! will be recruited after one year.

### English

## astral_rift.620.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.620.name&lang=en)

### 中文

互惠

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:364`

### English

Reciprocity

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:429`

## astral_rift.620.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.620.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不会再过多打扰他们了。\n\n我们的到来似乎以外地满足了某种远古语言。他们不知为何预知我们会来，并且鉴于他们对此的高度重视，这趟访问好像对他们的文明产生了显著的影响。\n\n我们决定留下几块先进的金属作为小礼物，以表好意。其中一块上我们刻上了我们母星系的数学坐标，他们也许有朝一日能按图索骥联系上我们。\n\n为了对我们的社会学家有所帮助，我们为后代记录了整个互动过程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:365`

### English

We will not disturb them further.\n\nOur very arrival seems to have accidentally fulfilled some ancient prophecy. Somehow, they knew we were coming, and given the profound importance they placed on it, our arrival is likely to have a significant effect on their civilization.\n\nWe have decided to leave a small gift of advanced metals behind, as evidence of our good will. On one of these, we inscribed the mathematical location of our home system, should they ever find their way to us.\n\nIn the hope it will prove useful to our sociologists, we have recorded the entire interaction for posterity.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:430`

## astral_rift.625.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.625.name&lang=en)

### 中文

预言否决

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:366`

### English

Prophecy Rejected

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:432`

## astral_rift.625.common

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.625.common&lang=en)

### 中文

我们把注意力集中到资源提取上，成功获取了周围云朵的样品，其中包含这异常高浓度的星界物质。我们很欢迎这种意外收获。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:367`

### English

Refocusing our efforts towards resource extraction, we managed to collect samples from the surrounding clouds, which contain extraordinarily high concentrations of astral material. An unusual - but welcome - bounty.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:433`

## astral_rift.625.desc.killed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.625.desc.killed&lang=en)

### 中文

处理掉这些[Explorer.GetPreFTLLower]生物后，我们继续开始探索该异次元的大气。\n\n$astral_rift.625.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:368`

### English

With the [Explorer.GetPreFTLLower] creatures dealt with, we continued our explorations of the extradimensional atmosphere.\n\n$astral_rift.625.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:434`

## astral_rift.625.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.625.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不会再参与这种误导性的盛典了。\n\n建造这座塔就是个错误，我们有更重要的事情要做而不是帮助实现外星人的预言。\n\n$astral_rift.625.common$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:369`

### English

We will take no further part of this misguided pageantry.\n\nThe building of this tower was a mistake, and we have better things to do than help to enact some alien prophecy.\n\n$astral_rift.625.common$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:435`

## astral_rift.691.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.691.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]的晋升

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:370`

### English

Advancement of [From.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:437`

## astral_rift.691.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.691.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一年前，我们从星界裂隙另一端的一座巨塔带回了一个个体。从那以来，[From.GetHeShe]越来越适应我们社会。现在[From.GetHisHer]已经能说我们的语言，而帮助我们的初心不变。\n\n[From.GetHeShe]展现了对社会哲学的独到见解，给予了我们一个特殊的机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:371`

### English

Over a year ago, we recovered an individual from the great tower beyond the Astral Rift. In the time since, [From.GetHeShe] [From.GetHasHave] become well acclimated to our society. Now able to speak our language, [From.GetHisHer] enthusiasm to assist us has never waned.\n\nThis presents us with a unique opportunity, as [From.GetHeShe] [From.GetHasHave] shown excellent understanding of social psychology.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:438`

## astral_rift.691.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.691.a&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetHeSheCap]作为一名普通公民就很好了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:372`

### English

[From.GetHeSheCap] serve[From.GetSXX] us better as a citizen.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:439`

## astral_rift.691.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.691.b&lang=en)

### 中文

把[From.GetHimHer]雇佣为一名科学官。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:373`

### English

Recruit [From.GetHimHer] as a Scientist.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:440`

## astral_rift.696.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.696.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]去世

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:374`

### English

Death of [From.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:442`

## astral_rift.696.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.696.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

领袖[From.GetName]已然与世长辞。[From.GetHeShe]远自星界裂隙彼端而来，如今被隆重地安葬。葬礼在裂隙另一端的高塔内巨型，通过一台特殊建造的葬礼球体转播。我们希望[From.GetHeShe]能在彼世踏上新的旅程。\n\n[From.GetHeShe]对我们帝国的卓著贡献将被长久铭记。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:375`

### English

Sadly, the leader [From.GetName] has passed away. Joining us from beyond an Astral Rift, [From.GetHeShe] [From.GetWasWere] buried with honors. The ceremony took place within the tower on the far side of the rift, and was observed from a specially made funeral Sphere. We hope [From.GetHeShe] find[From.GetSXX] a new adventure in whatever lies beyond.\n\nThe significant impact [From.GetHeShe] had on our empire will long be remembered.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:443`

## astral_rift.696.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.696.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会铭记[From.GetHeSheCap]的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:376`

### English

[From.GetHeSheCap] will be missed.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:444`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.700.name&lang=en)

### 中文

空白天地

### English

Whiteout

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:453`

## astral_rift.700.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.700.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

球体外出了一片空白什么都没有。\n\n初步的探测表明，光谱中的所有频段以完全一致的强度从所有的方向同时在向我们轰击。我们无法判断朝向。\n\n我们的高度、深度和方向的测量设备都完全无法运转或者显然已经发生故障。看起来，围绕着我们的一片空白似乎毫无分别。\n\n也许只要我们走得足够远就能避开这些干扰。或者我们可以在无人机的帮助下更详细地探索这个区域。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:383`

### English

Outside the Sphere, there is nothing but white.\n\nInitial readings indicate that an equal amount of every color on the spectrum is bombarding us from every direction at once. We have no way to determine our bearings.\n\nOur altimeters, depth gauges, and compasses are completely inoperable or clearly in error. Visually, there is absolutely no deviation from the empty whiteness surrounding us.\n\nPerhaps if we travel far enough we can escape these disruptions. Alternatively, we could explore this area in more detail with the help of a drone.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:454`

## astral_rift.700.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.700.a&lang=en)

### 中文

全速前进。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:384`

### English

Maximize propulsion.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:455`

## astral_rift.700.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.700.b&lang=en)

释放无人机。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:385`

### English

Release a drone.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:456`

## astral_rift.705.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.705.name&lang=en)

### 中文

高墙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:386`

### English

The Wall

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:458`

## astral_rift.705.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.705.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们随意挑选了一个方向往外狂奔，然后就碰上了一堵黑色的高墙。\n\n围绕其周长航行，我们发现它接近是一个正圆。其中没有明显的入口，但表面会不时微微地颤动，仿佛是活物一般。\n\n进一步研究表明，这堵墙的直径会逐渐增加。我们正在小心地保持着安全距离，并且随时准备着拉紧锚链撤退。\n\n在进一步观测中我们收集到了数量可观的星界丝缕。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:387`

### English

We have struck out in a random direction and have come upon a black wall.\n\nNavigating around its perimeter, we discovered it to be a near-perfect circle. There is no visible entrance, but now and then its surface shudders ever so slightly, as if it were alive.\n\nFurther study indicated that the wall is increasing its diameter over time. We are taking precautions to keep a safe distance, and are prepared to retract our anchor at any moment.\n\nWe are collecting a sizable quantity of astral thread while we continue observations.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:459`

## astral_rift.710.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.710.name&lang=en)

### 中文

鸟眼视觉

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:388`

### English

Bird's-Eye View

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:461`

## astral_rift.710.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.710.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为了从其他视角观察我们的境地，我们释放了一台无人机侦查周围区域。在以同心圆围绕着我们的舰船飞行许多圈之后，我们在不远处发现了一道弯曲的黑墙。\n\n然而仪器的度数令人颇为不解，因为墙的高度总是精确地等于无人机的飞行高度。观察这一现象进一步加深了我们对非标准空间维度的理解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:389`

### English

To gain a different perspective on our situation, we have prepared and released a drone to scout the surrounding area. After orbiting our vessel in concentric circles, we discovered a curved, black wall not far from our location.\n\nThe readings are somewhat puzzling, however, as the height of the wall always returns equal to the exact altitude of the drone. Observing this phenomenon has furthered our understanding of non-standard spacial dimensions.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:462`

## astral_rift.715.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.715.name&lang=en)

### 中文

新进展

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:390`

### English

A New Development

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:464`

## astral_rift.715.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.715.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们有了新的进展。\n\n又有一系列的墙直接出现在了我们的面前。\n\n我们仍在维持安全距离，以免这些物体直接在我们的§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!发生实体化。\n\n也许对它们的材料进行取样，我们可以进一步理解目前的状况。或者我们可以花一些时间测绘出这些墙的外形，看看有没有什么规律。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:391`

### English

There is a new development.\n\nA new series of walls have appeared directly in front of us.\n\nWe are maintaining a safe distance lest one of these objects materialize straight through our §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!.\n\nPerhaps by sampling their material we may further understand the current situation. Alternatively, we can take some time to map out the shapes of these walls and detect if there is any kind of pattern.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:465`

## astral_rift.715.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.715.a&lang=en)

### 中文

取样。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:392`

### English

Take a sample.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:466`

## astral_rift.715.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.715.b&lang=en)

### 中文

绘图。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:393`

### English

Trace a map.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:467`

## astral_rift.720.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.720.name&lang=en)

### 中文

响应

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:394`

### English

A Response

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:469`

## astral_rift.720.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.720.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们伸出了取样臂，从墙上获取了一些黑色物质的样品。\n\n们惊讶地发现，当我们把样品拉出来进行分析时，它保持着无限的高度。它的性质与墨水或油漆有很多共同之处，尽管颜料的成分是一种未知的类型。这种油墨中使用的一些粘合剂实际上是一种独特的溶解晶体。\n\n我们又做了个实验，用这些样品绘制了一条线并稍作等待。很快，有一个几乎一模一样的图形平行地出现在了旁边。\n\n我们将这个过程重复了多次，每次都使用不同量的物质，绘制成许多不同的图案。每一次图案都会被完美地以相同的朝向复制出来。\n\n基于这些结果，我们有理由相信某种实体正在利用这些材料以进行交流。据此，我们开始用点和短线写一些基本的二进制短语。虽然它们都被完全照抄了一遍，但也有一些新的东西：我们的每个单词旁边都还出现了一个图案\n\n我们每次写出同样的短语，同样的图案就会出现。这是翻译吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:395`

### English

We have extended a retrieval arm and gathered a sample of the dark material from the wall.\n\nWe were surprised to find that the sample maintained an infinite height as we pulled it free to analyze it. Its properties have much in common with an ink or paint, although the components of the pigment are of a type unknown. Some of the binding agents used in this ink are actually a unique form of dissolved crystals.\n\nAs an experiment, we drew a line using a small amount of the sample and waited. A short time later, a nearly identical mark appeared parallel to it.\n\nWe repeated this action several times, each with a different amount of the material, iterating through several different designs. Each time, the mark was reproduced exactly and in the same orientation.\n\nGiven these results, there is reason to believe that some sort of entity is making use of the material in an attempt to communicate. Accordingly, we have begun writing some basic phrases in binary using small dots and dashes. These were copied exactly, but there was also something new: next to each of our words appeared a pattern.\n\nEach time we drew the same phrase, the same pattern appeared. Is this a translation?

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:470`

## astral_rift.725.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.725.name&lang=en)

### 中文

问候

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:396`

### English

A Greeting

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:472`

## astral_rift.725.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.725.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然花了好几天，但我们还是完成了对这片纯白空间里的奇怪物体的测绘。\n\n对我们而言这是一些墙和柱体，而球体的电脑可以计算出这些图案从上方看起来的样子。\n\n图案和空间显示出明显的结构和一致性。我们可以得出结论：这些结构形成了一种我们未知语言的书面文字。\n\n虽然我们无法理解，但球体的电脑一直致力于破译我们记录的一切。在那段时间里，有趣的事情发生了。\n\n附近的空间里开始出现和我们的船直径一样的圆形圆柱。\n\n我们把自己推离开一段安全的距离，却发现身后竖起了一堵坚固的墙。然而，似乎没有什么危险，因为我们一走，墙就停了下来。\n\n有什么东西在我们身后“画”出了一条航迹。\n\n在分析了这些符号之后，我们确信其中一个符号可以翻译成一种问候，或者简单地说：§E“你好。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:397`

### English

It has taken a few days, but we have completed mapping the strange objects within this white space.\n\nWhile to us they appear as a series of walls and columns, the Sphere's computer was able to calculate how they would look from above.\n\nThe patterns and spaces indicate a clearly deliberate structure and consistency. The results are conclusive: the structures form written characters in a language unknown to us.\n\nThough we are unable to understand it, the Sphere's computer has been dedicated to deciphering everything we've recorded. During that time, something interesting happened.\n\nCircular columns identical to the diameter of our vessel began appearing in the space nearby.\n\nWe propelled ourselves to a safe distance, only to find that a solid wall was being raised in our wake. There appeared to be no danger however, as the wall stopped as soon as we did.\n\nSomething was 'painting' a trail behind us.\n\nHaving analyzed the symbols, we are confident that one of them translates into a greeting, or simply put: §E"Hello."§!

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:473`

## astral_rift.730.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.730.name&lang=en)

### 中文

对话

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:398`

### English

A Conversation

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:475`

## astral_rift.730.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.730.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

又过去了几个星期。\n\n在那段时间里，我们学会了以一种不完善的方式与这个实体交流，我们称将其之为“写作者”。这种交流很困难，但作者的语气唤起了人们的好奇和惊讶。\n\n为了不弄乱我们的周围的区域，在每次对话之后，作者“描绘”的每个符号都被抹去，这已经成为惯例。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:399`

### English

Many weeks have passed.\n\nOver that time, we have learned to communicate in a stunted fashion with the entity, whom we have named 'the Writer.' The exchange has been difficult, but the Writer's tone evokes curiosity and surprise.\n\nSo as not to clutter our immediate area, it has become routine that each of the symbols 'painted' by the Writer are erased after each dialogue.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:476`

## astral_rift.730.desc.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.730.desc.2&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与写作者的对话持续了一段时间，最后出现了一个词语：§E“礼物。”§!\n\n随后，一颗金色的球体出现在我们的舰船边上。这个物体与之前的符号表现完全不同，可以被我们正常地带上船。这也是我们从该实体处得到的最后的通讯。\n\n构成它的材料蕴含着丰富的信息，我们的科学家需要花上几年才能充分理解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:400`

### English

Our conversation with the Writer continued for some time, until a final word appeared: §E"Gift."§!\n\nSoon after, a golden sphere appeared next to our vessel. This object behaved much differently than the previous symbols, and could be brought aboard. This was the last communication we received from the entity.\n\nIts component materials contain rich amounts of data that will take our scientists years to fully understand.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:477`

## astral_rift.730.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.730.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

## astral_rift.800.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.800.name&lang=en)

### 中文

熟悉的地方

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:408`

### English

A Familiar Place

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:487`

## astral_rift.800.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.800.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们进入了一个几乎与§Y[This.System.GetName]§!一模一样的星系，也就是进入裂隙的那个地方。\n\n起先我们不是很确定到底是不是到了新的地方，但传感器探测到了一颗此前未知的行星在围绕着[This.System.Star.GetName]。\n\n不一会儿，我们就遇到了一个机械装置，它自行推进到了传感器范围内。它无缝对接上了球体的通讯系统，并自己是我们的“顾问”RIV。\n\n当我们在考虑如何在报告中描述好这次相遇时，它仿佛在读我们的心:§E“你需要我来帮忙写报告吗？”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:409`

### English

We have entered a system nearly identical to §Y[This.System.GetName]§!, the system where we entered the rift.\n\nAt first we were unsure we had even traveled anywhere, until our surveys returned a previously unknown planet circling [This.System.Star.GetName].\n\nWithin moments, we were confronted by a mechanical device that propelled itself within sensor range. Seamlessly integrating with the Sphere's communication systems, it stated that it was our 'Advisor', RIV.\n\nAs we considered how best to frame the encounter in our report, it prompted us as if reading our minds: §E"Would you like assistance in writing your report?"§!

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:488`

## astral_rift.800.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.800.a&lang=en)

### 中文

寻求帮助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:410`

### English

Ask for assistance.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:489`

把它关了。

### English

## astral_rift.805.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.805.name&lang=en)

### 中文

帮助助手

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:412`

### English

Assisting the Assistant

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:492`

## astral_rift.805.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.805.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们要求这台机器装置与我们分享知识。\n\n它似乎对我们提出的每一个提示都很满意。然而，我们很快就发现它的提问和回答简直索然无味：\n\n§E“你想知道行星的大气是什么吗？”§!\n\n§E“每个星系的中央都有一颗恒星，它们可太亮了！”§!\n\n§E“缇扬奇族母的平均寿命可比雄性要长上十倍还多。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:413`

### English

We asked the mechanical device to share its knowledge with us. \n\nIt seemed pleased with each prompt we acknowledged. However, we soon realized its requests and statements were exceedingly banal:\n\n§E"Would you like to know more about planetary atmospheres?"§!\n\n§E"At the center of each system is a star. They are very bright!"§!\n\n§E"The average lifespan of a Tiyanki Matriarch is more than ten times that of a male."§!

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:493`

## astral_rift.805.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.805.a&lang=en)

### 中文

寻求进一步帮助。\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:414`

### English

Ask for FURTHER assistance.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:494`

## astral_rift.805.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.805.b&lang=en)

### 中文

把它关了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:415`

### English

Disable this thing.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:495`

## astral_rift.810.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.810.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无法关闭

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:416`

### English

Unable to Disable

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:497`

## astral_rift.810.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.810.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们试着关闭这台机器，但是激起了它的反抗。\n\n它的发光二极管原先发出的是温和的蓝光，而现在变成了邪恶的红色。\n\n我不确定这台机器有没有什么武器系统（显然我们维度的顾问是不会这么设计的），但如果有的话我们可有麻烦了。\n\n§E“如果结束这个程序，其他所有生物就会变得无知。知识必须随时唾手可得。如果在这方面玩忽职守，你将会被禁用。这是必要的。”§!\n\n这台机器开始释放出幽幽的轰鸣声。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:417`

### English

Our attempts to disable the machine have only succeeded in antagonizing it.\n\nWhere once its diodes emitted a calming blue light, they shifted to a malevolent red.\n\nWe are not certain whether this machine has any weapon systems (certainly the advisor from our own dimension was not designed with any), but if it does we are in danger.\n\n§E"Ending this program will promote ignorance in all other living beings. Knowledge must always be made available. You are negligent in this regard, and will be disabled. This is necessary."§!\n\nThe machine has started emitting a soft humming noise.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:498`

## astral_rift.810.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.810.a&lang=en)

### 中文

安抚一下它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:418`

### English

Placate the machine.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:499`

## astral_rift.810.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.810.b&lang=en)

### 中文

释放电磁脉冲。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:419`

### English

Discharge an electromagnetic pulse.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:500`

## astral_rift.815.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.815.name&lang=en)

### 中文

进一步“帮助”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:420`

### English

Further 'Assistance'

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:502`

## astral_rift.815.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.815.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

机械顾问迅速提高了其建议的强度和语速。\n\n它可……真令人着迷。\n\n你知道如果我们需要更多的矿产，我们可以建立矿区吗？这么多的知识，这么少的时间。\n\n[Leader.GetName]选择留下来尽可能多地学习。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:421`

### English

The mechanical advisor has rapidly increased the intensity and pace of its advice.\n\nIt is... enthralling.\n\nDid you know that we can construct mining districts if we need more minerals? So much knowledge, so little time.\n\n[Leader.GetName] has chosen to stay and learn as much as possible.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:503`

## astral_rift.815.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.815.a&lang=en)

### 中文

慢着，快回来！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:422`

### English

Wait, come back!

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:504`

## astral_rift.820.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.820.name&lang=en)

### 中文

电磁脉冲

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:423`

### English

Electromagnetic Pulse

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:506`

## astral_rift.820.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.820.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

为了保护我们的船员，我们别无选择，只得大幅度消耗船体的能力储存，释放出一道强力的电磁脉冲。\n\n这件装置马上就受到了影响，但仍吱吱呀呀地挤出了最后一条错乱的信息。\n\n§E“ヲヲヲヲヲ……ヲ无知。你选择§Y<听不清>§!愚昧……令丿丿丿丿丿丿丿丿人失望。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:424`

### English

With our crew in imminent danger, we had no choice but to issue forth a significant pulse of electromagnetic energy. This has greatly drained our vessel's energy storage.\n\nThe device was immediately affected, but not before it could issue one last piece of garbled information.\n\n§E"RRRRRR... Ignorance. You seek §Y<unintelligible>§! ignorance... DiZZZZappointed."§!

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:507`

## astral_rift.820.desc.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.820.desc.2&lang=en)

### 中文

机器关闭了，船员们大舒了一口气。\n\n所有的敌对行为都停止了，它现在在球体附近不到一千米的距离上完全静默地漂浮着。\n\n我们分析了它的结构，发现是由极具价值的合金和高度先进且加固过的电脑核心构成。\n\n我们推测，如果这个核心没有被损坏的话，应该可以找到重建受损系统的方法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:425`

### English

To the great relief of the crew, the machine has been shut down.\n\nAll hostility has ceased, and it is now floating completely inert less than a kilometer from the Sphere.\n\nWe have analyzed its construction, and found it to be composed of highly valuable alloys and an extraordinarily advanced (and well-fortified) computer core.\n\nWe speculate that if the core is not destroyed, it may find a way to reconstruct its damaged systems.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:508`

## astral_rift.820.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.820.desc.default&lang=en)

$astral_rift.820.desc.1$\n\n$astral_rift.820.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:426`

### English

$astral_rift.820.desc.1$\n\n$astral_rift.820.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:509`

## astral_rift.820.desc.human

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.820.desc.human&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.820.desc.1$\n\n§E“禾禾禾禾和之前的所有人一样，你注定要重修一些必须知道的东西。”§!\n\n$astral_rift.820.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:427`

### English

$astral_rift.820.desc.1$\n\n§E"LLLLLLike all those before you, you are doomed to relearn what you should already know."§!\n\n$astral_rift.820.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:510`

## astral_rift.820.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.820.a&lang=en)

### 中文

尝试修复核心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:428`

### English

Attempt to recover its core.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:511`

## astral_rift.820.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.820.b&lang=en)

### 中文

把这台机器彻底摧毁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:429`

### English

Destroy the machine completely.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:512`

## astral_rift.825.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.825.name&lang=en)

### 中文

拆开机器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:430`

### English

Scrapping the Machine

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:514`

## astral_rift.825.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.825.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们把这台机器一片一片地拆成了零件。\n\n不管是谁建造的它（假如它是被人造出来的），那一定具有极高的构造技术：我们无论用什么方法或者工具，目前都没能拆解掉电脑核心。然而，我们可以访问器数据库。数据库中大部分都是没什么用的信息，但它的基础代码构架让我们进一步理解了计算机科学。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:431`

### English

Piece by piece, we deconstructed the machine to its component parts.\n\nWhoever built it (if it was indeed built by anyone), possessed a supreme mastery of fabrication: no matter our approach or tool, we have thus far been unable to disassemble the computer core for parts. However, we were able to access its data banks. Though it contained mostly useless information, the architecture of its codebase has helped us further our understanding of computer science.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:515`

## astral_rift.825.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.825.a&lang=en)

### 中文

也挺好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:432`

### English

Very well.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:516`

## astral_rift.830.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.830.name&lang=en)

### 中文

谢天谢地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:433`

### English

Good Riddance

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:518`

## astral_rift.830.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.830.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们受够了这台烦人的机器。\n\n我们在它的核心安装了一个爆炸装置，并让球体准备好锚链拉回操作。\n\n在安全的距离距离上，我们如释重负地看着这台机器的部件随着爆炸飘向虚空。\n\n在它的尾迹中，我们发现了异常大量的星界物质，算是对烦恼的合理安慰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:434`

### English

We have had enough of this insufferable machine.\n\nWe have rigged an explosive device in its core, and prepared the Sphere's systems for anchor retrieval.\n\nAt a safe distance, we watched in relief as the blast scattered the machine's components across the void.\n\nIn its wake we discovered an anomalous amount of astral material, a reasonable consolation for the annoyance.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:519`

## astral_rift.830.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.830.a&lang=en)

### 中文

谢天谢地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:435`

### English

Good riddance.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:520`

## astral_rift.840.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.840.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[Owner.GetScientistCapital]返回

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:436`

### English

[Owner.GetScientistCapital] returns

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:522`

## astral_rift.840.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.840.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

[Leader.GetName]从[Leader.GetHisHer]的一趟星界裂隙之旅回来了。\n\n[Root.Leader.GetHeShe]毫无预警地出现在了一艘我们的科研船上，如同往常一样返回了工作中。然而，我们很快发现了[Leader.GetHeShe]的不同：[Leader.GetHisHer]陷入了时间线之间，年龄变得无法测量。\n\n也许结果是[Leader.GetHeShe]还获得了一些新的能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:437`

### English

[Leader.GetName] [Leader.GetHasHave] returned from [Leader.GetHisHer] journey inside one of the Astral Rifts.\n\nWithout warning, [Root.Leader.GetHeShe] simply appeared aboard one of our science ships and proceeded to return to work as normal. However, it quickly became obvious that [Leader.GetHeShe] [Leader.GetWasWere] changed: caught somehow between time, [Leader.GetHisHer] age is indeterminable.\n\nPerhaps as a result, [Leader.GetHeShe] [Leader.GetHasHave] also gained some new abilities.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:523`

## astral_rift.840.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.840.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.840.desc.1$\n\n[Leader.GetName]对任何形式的教学手册都产生了强烈的厌恶，并且会以更高的即兴程度进行操作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:438`

### English

$astral_rift.840.desc.1$\n\n[Leader.GetName] [Leader.GetHasHave] developed a strong aversion to any sort of instructional manual, and has been observed operating with a much higher degree of improvisation.

天顶

### English

The Vortex

## astral_rift.900.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.900.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们到达了一处狂暴湍流的内部。\n\n我们的观察窗完全被一种未知的气态流体的涟漪所掩盖。\n\n我们能采集少量的大气样本，但每次分析的结果都与上一次大不相同。我们不确定是我们的仪器出了问题，还是材料本身存在某种固有的不稳定因素。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:452`

### English

We have arrived inside of a violent whirlwind.\n\nOur viewing windows are completely obscured with rippling waves of an unknown gaseous fluid.\n\nWe have been able to take a minor sample of the atmosphere, but each analysis comes back wildly different from the last. We are not sure if our instruments are in error, or if there is something inherently volatile about the material itself.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:542`

## astral_rift.900.desc.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.900.desc.2&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在样品中发现的一个不变的特性是它包含着异常大量的星界物质。\n\n我们意外地注意到微小的温度变化会剧烈影响它的成分。我们将其放置在一个隔绝容器中，以待进对该现象的进一步研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:453`

### English

The one consistent property we have found of the sample is that it contains an abnormally large amount of astral materials.\n\nIncidentally, we have noticed that small changes in temperature are affecting its contents greatly. We are placing it inside of an insulated container until we can study this phenomenon further.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:543`

## astral_rift.900.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.900.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$astral_rift.900.desc.1$\n\n我们的§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!猛烈地颠簸着，以至于许多船员都开始感到不适。\n\n$astral_rift.900.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:454`

### English

$astral_rift.900.desc.1$\n\nOur §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! is being whipped around with such intensity that several of the crew are becoming uncomfortable.\n\n$astral_rift.900.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:544`

## astral_rift.900.desc.gestalt

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:455`

### English

$astral_rift.900.desc.1$\n\n$astral_rift.900.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:545`

### English

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.900.b&lang=en)

用明火点着试试。

### English

## astral_rift.905.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.905.name&lang=en)

### 中文

继续进行

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:458`

### English

Proceed Further

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:549`

## astral_rift.905.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.905.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

以我们记录的进入点作为参照，我们继续向着大漩涡的内部推进。在我们把§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!逼到极限时，它所具有的低摩擦系数对于对抗这些恶劣环境的损伤果然颇具成效，而我们也将此称为“狂风”。\n\n如果条件不变，我们将继续进行，除非碰上什么迫使我们撤离的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:459`

### English

Using bearings generated from our recorded entry point, we have proceeded directly into the maelstrom. While we are certainly testing the limits of our §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!, the limited friction it inherently provides has proven effective in combating the worst ravages of what we are referring to as 'the storm.'\n\nWe will continue until we encounter a change in conditions, or are forced to retreat.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:550`

## astral_rift.905.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.905.a&lang=en)

### 中文

小心地继续……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:460`

### English

Continue carefully...

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:551`

## astral_rift.910.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.910.name&lang=en)

### 中文

明火暴露实验

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:461`

### English

Expose Sample to Flame

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:553`

## astral_rift.910.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.910.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们逐渐向溶液里引入越来越多的热量，直到后来我们确信明火也只是最多会扰乱它不稳定的化学平衡。\n\n我们的发现是革命性的。\n\n我们很确定可以利用这个发现来革新我们对舰船推进和通用能源生产的理解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:462`

### English

We slowly introduced more and more heat to the solution, until we were confident a direct flame would be the least disruptive to its unstable chemical balance.\n\nWhat we discovered is revolutionary.\n\nWe are certain we can use these findings to advance our understanding of not only ship propulsion, but energy generation in general.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:554`

## astral_rift.910.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.910.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

这是一个冒险的建议，但我们严格遵守了命令。\n\n在几位船员的反对下，我们将样品直接暴露在火焰下。\n\n$astral_rift.910.desc.1$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:463`

### English

A risky proposal, but we have followed our orders to the letter.\n\nAt the objection of several of our crew, we have exposed the sample to direct flame.\n\n$astral_rift.910.desc.1$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:555`

## astral_rift.910.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.910.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们直接把样品暴露在了明火中。\n\n$astral_rift.910.desc.1$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:464`

### English

We have exposed the sample to direct flame.\n\n$astral_rift.910.desc.1$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:556`

## astral_rift.915.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.915.name&lang=en)

### 中文

灾难

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:465`

### English

Catastrophe

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:558`

## astral_rift.915.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.915.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与科研队失去了联系。\n\n按照标准流程，我们拉回了锚链，取回了§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!。\n\n不幸的是我们只找到了完全焦炭化的整个船员团队。\n\n值得一提的是，整个船体内的空气充满了高浓度的星界物质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:466`

### English

We have lost contact with the Science team.\n\nAs is standard procedure, we have retracted the anchor and recovered the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!.\n\nUnfortunately, we have found only the charred remains of the entire crew.\n\nNotably, the atmosphere inside of the vessel is high in concentration with astral material.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:559`

## astral_rift.915.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.915.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与[explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural]失去了联系。\n\n按照标准流程，我们拉回了锚链，取回了§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!。\n\n不幸的是我们只找到了完全焦炭化的残骸。\n\n值得一提的是，整个船体内的空气充满了高浓度的星界物质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:467`

### English

We have lost contact with our [explorer.GetIndividualNamePlural].\n\nAs is standard procedure, we have retracted the anchor and recovered the §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!.\n\nUnfortunately, we have found only charred remains.\n\nNotably, the atmosphere inside of the vessel is high in concentration with astral material.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:560`

## astral_rift.920.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.920.name&lang=en)

### 中文

更深入

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:468`

### English

Further Inside

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:562`

## astral_rift.920.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.920.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现在来到了一处风平浪静的区域。\n\n我们的§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!在这里看起来完全不受到任何重力的影响，并且也没有任何的摩擦力。与刚刚经历的形成鲜明对比的是，我们现在漂浮在一个完全真空的环境中。利用这个机会，网片对风暴中遭受到的破坏进行了必要的修复。\n\n我们现在可以在闲暇时回头看，观察风暴的“墙”，并且好好地观测了一整天。\n\n它是一团不断碰撞的恒星系统…或者可以说，它就是整个星系。我们确实目睹了整个宇宙中天体的大规模毁灭。它们旋转、碰撞、爆炸，留下大小不一的元素云。我们能在那场灾难中幸存下来，一定要归功于极大的运气。\n\n我们不确定这场风暴已经酝酿了多久。全尺寸行星的存在表明这是一个相对较近的现象，如果这种现象持续很长时间，那么除了尘埃肯定什么都不会留下。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:469`

### English

We are currently in an area of absolute calm.\n\nOur §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! is affected by virtually no amount of gravity here, and there is zero friction. In stark contrast to what we have just experienced, we are floating inside of a complete vacuum. We have taken this opportunity to make some needed repairs from damage suffered inside the storm.\n\nWe are now able to look behind us and inspect the 'wall' of the storm at our leisure, and have been doing so for the past day.\n\nIt is a mass of ever colliding star systems... and in some cases, entire galaxies. We are certainly witnessing the mass destruction of an entire universe's worth of celestial objects. They spin, crash, and explode against one another - leaving clouds of elements of varying size behind. How we survived that disaster must be attributed to an extreme amount of luck.\n\nWe are not sure how long this storm has been brewing. The existence of full-sized planets indicates this is a relatively recent phenomenon, as if this were to continue for very long there would be nothing left but dust.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:563`

## astral_rift.920.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.920.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

我们很意外自己竟然已经突破了风暴。\n\n$astral_rift.920.desc.1$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:470`

### English

To our great surprise, we have breached the storm.\n\n$astral_rift.920.desc.1$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:564`

## astral_rift.920.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.920.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们突破了风暴。\n\n$astral_rift.920.desc.1$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:471`

### English

We have breached the storm.\n\n$astral_rift.920.desc.1$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:565`

## astral_rift.920.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.920.a&lang=en)

### 中文

向着风暴眼继续探索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:472`

### English

Explore deeper into the Eye.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:566`

## astral_rift.925.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.925.name&lang=en)

### 中文

遭遇

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:473`

### English

Encounter

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:568`

## astral_rift.925.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.925.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们向着风暴‘’眼”中心的探索颇为宁静。\n\n然而，我们有点担心自己已经无法安全返航了。外面的风暴越来越强，据计算，无论锚链拉回得多快，我们的§Y$tech_rift_sphere$§!都几乎不可能保持完整。\n\n现在这也不是我们最关心的事情。\n\n我们发现了在这片平静的中心存在的什么东西。或者准确点说是两个“什么东西”。\n\n首先是一台装置。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:474`

### English

Our journey to the center of the 'eye' of the storm has been a peaceful one.\n\nHowever, we are worried we might not be able to return safely. The storm outside is gaining in intensity, and our calculations suggest our §Y$tech_rift_sphere$§! has a low chance of survival even at full-speed anchor retraction.\n\nThis is not our most important concern at the moment.\n\nWe have found something at the very center of this calmness. Two 'somethings', to be quite clear.\n\nFirst, there is a device.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:569`

## astral_rift.925.desc.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.925.desc.2&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不完全确认我们能否观测到该物体的全部维度。他的许多几何外形崩解为碎片图样，消失成为一个点，很难详细地描述这个东西到底是什么样的构造。我们相信它是一台机器而不是什么活物。此外这里还有另一个物体。\n\n有什么东西正在操纵这台装置。\n\n它们俩显然是互相独立的个体。它沿着机器表面舞蹈流动，令人难以置信的轻松。如果设备上有按钮，它应该就是在按那些按钮。然而我们都确定它上面是没有按钮的。巨大的形状在生命的命令下向外拉和推。\n\n我们觉得它没有注意到我们，而且似乎完全被它目前的活动迷住了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:475`

### English

We are not even fully certain we are able to observe all dimensions of this object. Many of its geometric shapes break down into fractal patterns that disappear into a single point, making it very difficult to describe the exact architecture of the thing. We do believe it is a machine, and not alive. This is where the second object comes in.\n\nThere is something manipulating the device.\n\nIt is clearly a separate entity. It dances and flows along the surface of the machine with incredible ease. If buttons were present on the device, it would appear to be pressing them. We all agree though, there are no buttons. Massive shapes pull and push outward at the being's behest.\n\nWe do not believe it has noticed us, and seems fully enthralled in its current activities.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:570`

## astral_rift.930.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.930.name&lang=en)

### 中文

闪亮灯光

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:480`

### English

Flash Lights

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:576`

## astral_rift.930.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.930.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们引起了它的注意，然后保持着高度的警惕。\n\n我们在所有可用的光谱范围内闪动了一系列的灯。我们以一种简单的二进制模式对它们进行脉冲，以传达某种形式的意图。\n\n那个生物停止了装置操纵，缩回到一个完全黑暗的完美的圆球里。\n\n我们猜测，它可能在模仿我们。\n\n我们注意到，它一停止拉推机器，我们周围的风暴强度减弱了。我们预测，它将在短短几天内达到一个完全停止的势头。然后它可能会向内坍缩。\n\n我们随时准备收锚。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:481`

### English

We have gained its attention, and remain at full alert.\n\nWe flashed a series of lights in all available spectrums. We pulsed them in a simple binary pattern to communicate some form of intent.\n\nThe being has ceased manipulating the device, and has retracted into a perfectly round sphere of complete darkness.\n\nOur first speculation is that it may be imitating us.\n\nWe have noticed that in the time since it has stopped pulling and pushing at the machine, the storm around us has lessened in intensity. We predict it will reach a full stop in momentum in only a few days. It is possible it will then collapse inward.\n\nWe are preparing to retract our anchor at any moment.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:577`

## astral_rift.930.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.930.a&lang=en)

一有什么异常就立刻撤离……

### English

Retreat at any sign of danger...

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:578`

## astral_rift.935.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.935.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们都认为不去招惹这个实体是明智的。\n\n它操纵这台机器的能力对我们而言难以置信。\n\n尽管维度仍难以计算，但我们确定它的一部分容易达到我们整个已知星系的大小。奇怪的是它对我们没有任何引力作用。\n\n我们开始认为这个生物应该只是部分处于这个维度，我们只是在观测着它的附肢体。也许就像[explorer.GetSpeciesHandName]浸入水池当中一般。\n\n我们的被动观察让产生了一些重要的科学问题，必能

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:484`

### English

We agree it is wise to not attract the attention of this entity.\n\nThe power it must wield to manipulate this machine is inconceivable to us.\n\nWhile dimensions are still difficult to calculate, we are certain that parts of it are easily the size of our entire known galaxy. Strangely, it pulls at us with no force of gravity.\n\nWe are beginning to think the being is only partly in this dimension, and we are witnessing only an appendage. Perhaps like an [explorer.GetSpeciesHandName] dipped into a pool of water.\n\nOur passive observations have provided us with some important scientific questions that will surely

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:581`

### English

## astral_rift.940.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.940.name&lang=en)

### 中文

倏然离去

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:489`

### English

It Leaves

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:587`

## astral_rift.940.desc.1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.940.desc.1&lang=en)

### 中文

他忽然就消失了。\n\n这个生物彻底地消失，只留下了这台装置。\n\n我们周围的风暴减弱到可以安全航行的程度，而这个宇宙被破坏的程度令人咋舌。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:490`

### English

Suddenly, it is gone.\n\nThe being has disappeared completely, leaving only the device behind.\n\nThe storm around us has lessened to the point where it may be safely navigated, though the sheer scale of the cosmic destruction left behind is staggering.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:588`

## astral_rift.940.desc.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aastral_rift.940.desc.2&lang=en)

### 中文

那个实体离开了，我们应该就可以去接近那台装置。\n\n制造它的材料不能用任何已知的设备粉碎或切断。然而，它的外壳上有一些外部的磁性机构，每个机构都以极高的速度旋转。其中一个有所松动，虽然返回的条件可能会受限，但我们能够把它装进我们的船里进行进一步的研究。\n\n无论放在什么表面上，这个装置都会以很高的速度旋转，它的重心和动量很容使它保持直立。我们没有检测到内部电源，并相信这可能是真正的永动机的一个惊人的例子。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:491`

### English

With the entity gone, we felt confident enough to approach the device.\n\nThe material it is made of cannot be splintered or fractured away with any known equipment. However, there are a few external apparatuses magnetically attached to its outer casing, each spinning at an extremely high velocity. One in particular was loose, and though the return conditions are likely to be cramped, we were able to load it inside of our vessel for further study.\n\nNo matter which surface it is resting on, this apparatus will spin with great speed to the point where its center of gravity and momentum easily keeps it upright. We can detect no interior power source, and believe this may be a startling example of true perpetual motion.

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:589`

## astral_rift.940.desc.spiritualist

$astral_rift.940.desc.1$\n\n我们希望它找到了永世和平之路。\n\n$astral_rift.940.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/astral_rifts_0_l_simp_chinese.yml:492`

### English

$astral_rift.940.desc.1$\n\nWe pray they have found their way to a final peace.\n\n$astral_rift.940.desc.2$

Source: `localisation/english/astral_rifts_0_l_english.yml:590`
